### 词群 01.01｜地球的圈层与内部结构 {#group-01-01}

**串联方式：** 先从外部圈层看地球，再进入地核、地壳和地幔；辨认不同层级的位置。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">atmosphere<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-001" aria-label="定位 atmosphere">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-001" data-mark-label="atmosphere" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="atmosphere：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="atmosphere：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=atmosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 atmosphere" title="在有道查询 atmosphere">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 大气层，大气圈；气氛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Water moves between the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=atmosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>atmosphere</strong></a>, hydrosphere and lithosphere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">水在大气圈、水圈和岩石圈之间移动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Pollution can pass from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=atmosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>atmosphere</strong></a> into the hydrosphere and eventually the lithosphere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">污染物可能从大气圈进入水圈，最终进入岩石圈。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists model exchanges of heat among the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=atmosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>atmosphere</strong></a>, hydrosphere and lithosphere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家模拟大气圈、水圈与岩石圈之间的热量交换。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the Earth's atmosphere</span> · 地球大气层</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">biosphere</a></span> · 生物圈</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Earth%20system&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Earth system</a></span> · 地球系统</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-002">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-002" class="vocabulary-word-title">hydrosphere<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-002" aria-label="定位 hydrosphere">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-002" data-mark-label="hydrosphere" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hydrosphere：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hydrosphere：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hydrosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hydrosphere" title="在有道查询 hydrosphere">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-002</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 水圈；地球上水的总体</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Water moves between the atmosphere, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hydrosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hydrosphere</strong></a> and lithosphere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">水在大气圈、水圈和岩石圈之间移动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Pollution can pass from the atmosphere into the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hydrosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hydrosphere</strong></a> and eventually the lithosphere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">污染物可能从大气圈进入水圈，最终进入岩石圈。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists model exchanges of heat among the atmosphere, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hydrosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hydrosphere</strong></a> and lithosphere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家模拟大气圈、水圈与岩石圈之间的热量交换。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the global hydrosphere</span> · 全球水圈</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">biosphere</a></span> · 生物圈</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Earth%20system&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Earth system</a></span> · 地球系统</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-003">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-003" class="vocabulary-word-title">lithosphere<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-003" aria-label="定位 lithosphere">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-003" data-mark-label="lithosphere" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="lithosphere：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="lithosphere：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lithosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 lithosphere" title="在有道查询 lithosphere">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-003</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 岩石圈</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Water moves between the atmosphere, hydrosphere and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lithosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lithosphere</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">水在大气圈、水圈和岩石圈之间移动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Pollution can pass from the atmosphere into the hydrosphere and eventually the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lithosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lithosphere</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">污染物可能从大气圈进入水圈，最终进入岩石圈。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists model exchanges of heat among the atmosphere, hydrosphere and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lithosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lithosphere</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家模拟大气圈、水圈与岩石圈之间的热量交换。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the upper lithosphere</span> · 上部岩石圈</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biosphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">biosphere</a></span> · 生物圈</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Earth%20system&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Earth system</a></span> · 地球系统</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-008">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-008" class="vocabulary-word-title">core<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-008" aria-label="定位 core">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-008" data-mark-label="core" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="core：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="core：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=core&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 core" title="在有道查询 core">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-008</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 地核；核心；其他语境可指果核</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The crust lies above the mantle, which surrounds the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=core&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>core</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地壳位于地幔上方，地幔包围着地核。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The model shows the thin crust, thick mantle and central <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=core&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>core</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模型展示了薄薄的地壳、厚厚的地幔以及中心的地核。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Seismic waves reveal differences between the crust, mantle and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=core&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>core</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地震波揭示了地壳、地幔与地核之间的差异。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the Earth's core</span> · 地核</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tectonics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tectonics</a></span> · 地壳构造</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=layer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">layer</a></span> · 层</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-009">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-009" class="vocabulary-word-title">crust<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-009" aria-label="定位 crust">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-009" data-mark-label="crust" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="crust：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="crust：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 crust" title="在有道查询 crust">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-009</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 地壳；外壳</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crust</strong></a> lies above the mantle, which surrounds the core.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地壳位于地幔上方，地幔包围着地核。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The model shows the thin <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crust</strong></a>, thick mantle and central core.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模型展示了薄薄的地壳、厚厚的地幔以及中心的地核。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Seismic waves reveal differences between the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crust</strong></a>, mantle and core.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地震波揭示了地壳、地幔与地核之间的差异。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the Earth's crust</span> · 地壳</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tectonics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tectonics</a></span> · 地壳构造</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=layer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">layer</a></span> · 层</li></ul>

### 词群 01.02｜圈层中的气体与化合物 {#group-01-02}

**串联方式：** 给大气中的成分命名，再区分元素与氧化物；这些词可接到植物呼吸和气候问题。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-004">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-004" class="vocabulary-word-title">oxygen<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-004" aria-label="定位 oxygen">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-004" data-mark-label="oxygen" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="oxygen：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="oxygen：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oxygen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 oxygen" title="在有道查询 oxygen">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-004</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 氧气</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Hydrogen reacts with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oxygen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oxygen</strong></a> to form water, while burning carbon produces the oxide known as carbon dioxide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">氢与氧反应生成水，而碳燃烧产生称为二氧化碳的氧化物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike carbon dioxide, a carbon oxide, water consists of hydrogen and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oxygen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oxygen</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与碳的氧化物二氧化碳不同，水由氢和氧组成。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The chart compares hydrogen and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oxygen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oxygen</strong></a> with the compounds carbon dioxide and iron oxide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">图表将氢和氧与二氧化碳、氧化铁这两种化合物进行比较。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 01.03｜给地球定位 {#group-01-03}

**串联方式：** 先辨地球、半球与两极，再用经纬度、海拔和地平线描述位置。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-011">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-011" class="vocabulary-word-title">longitude<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-011" aria-label="定位 longitude">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-011" data-mark-label="longitude" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="longitude：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="longitude：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=longitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 longitude" title="在有道查询 longitude">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-011</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 经度</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey recorded <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=longitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>longitude</strong></a>, latitude and altitude before measuring the sun above the horizon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查先记录经度、纬度及海拔，再测量太阳在地平线以上的高度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At this latitude and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=longitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>longitude</strong></a>, the hill's altitude gives observers a clear view of the horizon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个经纬度位置，这座山的海拔使观察者可以清晰看到地平线。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Enter your <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=longitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>longitude</strong></a>, latitude and altitude to predict where the moon will rise above the horizon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">输入经度、纬度及海拔，即可预测月亮从地平线升起的位置。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">longitude and latitude</span> · 经纬度</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coordinate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coordinate</a></span> · 坐标</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elevation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">elevation</a></span> · 海拔</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-012">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-012" class="vocabulary-word-title">latitude<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-012" aria-label="定位 latitude">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-012" data-mark-label="latitude" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="latitude：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="latitude：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=latitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 latitude" title="在有道查询 latitude">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-012</span></span></summary>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey recorded longitude, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=latitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>latitude</strong></a> and altitude before measuring the sun above the horizon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查先记录经度、纬度及海拔，再测量太阳在地平线以上的高度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=latitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>latitude</strong></a> and longitude, the hill's altitude gives observers a clear view of the horizon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个经纬度位置，这座山的海拔使观察者可以清晰看到地平线。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Enter your longitude, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=latitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>latitude</strong></a> and altitude to predict where the moon will rise above the horizon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">输入经度、纬度及海拔，即可预测月亮从地平线升起的位置。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-013">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 地平线；[~s] 眼界，见识</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey recorded longitude, latitude and altitude before measuring the sun above the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horizon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>horizon</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查先记录经度、纬度及海拔，再测量太阳在地平线以上的高度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At this latitude and longitude, the hill's altitude gives observers a clear view of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horizon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>horizon</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个经纬度位置，这座山的海拔使观察者可以清晰看到地平线。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Enter your longitude, latitude and altitude to predict where the moon will rise above the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horizon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>horizon</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">输入经度、纬度及海拔，即可预测月亮从地平线升起的位置。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-014">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey recorded longitude, latitude and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=altitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>altitude</strong></a> before measuring the sun above the horizon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查先记录经度、纬度及海拔，再测量太阳在地平线以上的高度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At this latitude and longitude, the hill's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=altitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>altitude</strong></a> gives observers a clear view of the horizon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个经纬度位置，这座山的海拔使观察者可以清晰看到地平线。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Enter your longitude, latitude and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=altitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>altitude</strong></a> to predict where the moon will rise above the horizon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">输入经度、纬度及海拔，即可预测月亮从地平线升起的位置。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">at high altitude</span> · 在高海拔处</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coordinate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coordinate</a></span> · 坐标</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elevation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">elevation</a></span> · 海拔</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-064">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-064" class="vocabulary-word-title">globe<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-064" aria-label="定位 globe">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-064" data-mark-label="globe" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="globe：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="globe：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=globe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 globe" title="在有道查询 globe">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-064</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 球体；地球仪；[the ~] 地球</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=globe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>globe</strong></a> shows how the equator separates one hemisphere from the other.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地球仪展示了赤道如何将两个半球分开。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Find the equator on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=globe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>globe</strong></a>, then locate Australia in the southern hemisphere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在地球仪上找到赤道，再找出南半球的澳大利亚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">On this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=globe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>globe</strong></a>, each hemisphere is labelled above or below the equator.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这个地球仪在赤道上方和下方分别标明了各半球。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a classroom globe</span> · 教室里的地球仪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=meridian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">meridian</a></span> · 子午线</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tropics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tropics</a></span> · 热带地区</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-065">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-065" class="vocabulary-word-title">hemisphere<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-065" aria-label="定位 hemisphere">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-065" data-mark-label="hemisphere" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hemisphere：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hemisphere：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hemisphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hemisphere" title="在有道查询 hemisphere">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-065</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 半球</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The globe shows how the equator separates one <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hemisphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hemisphere</strong></a> from the other.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地球仪展示了赤道如何将两个半球分开。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Find the equator on the globe, then locate Australia in the southern <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hemisphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hemisphere</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在地球仪上找到赤道，再找出南半球的澳大利亚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">On this globe, each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hemisphere&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hemisphere</strong></a> is labelled above or below the equator.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这个地球仪在赤道上方和下方分别标明了各半球。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the northern hemisphere</span> · 北半球</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=meridian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">meridian</a></span> · 子午线</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tropics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tropics</a></span> · 热带地区</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-066">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-066" class="vocabulary-word-title">equator<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-066" aria-label="定位 equator">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-066" data-mark-label="equator" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="equator：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="equator：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 equator" title="在有道查询 equator">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-066</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 赤道</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The globe shows how the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>equator</strong></a> separates one hemisphere from the other.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地球仪展示了赤道如何将两个半球分开。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Find the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>equator</strong></a> on the globe, then locate Australia in the southern hemisphere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在地球仪上找到赤道，再找出南半球的澳大利亚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">On this globe, each hemisphere is labelled above or below the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>equator</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这个地球仪在赤道上方和下方分别标明了各半球。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">cross the equator</span> · 越过赤道</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=meridian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">meridian</a></span> · 子午线</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tropics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tropics</a></span> · 热带地区</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-067">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-067" class="vocabulary-word-title">arctic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-067" aria-label="定位 arctic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-067" data-mark-label="arctic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="arctic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="arctic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arctic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 arctic" title="在有道查询 arctic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-067</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 北极的（地理专名常写 Arctic）；n. 北极地区</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The Earth's axis runs through each pole, linking the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arctic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Arctic</strong></a> and Antarctic polar regions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地轴穿过两极，连接北极与南极的极地区域。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tilted axis explains seasonal daylight around each pole in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arctic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Arctic</strong></a> and Antarctic polar zones.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">倾斜的地轴解释了北极和南极各极点周围极地区域的季节性日照。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The diagram marks each pole on the axis and compares <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arctic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Arctic</strong></a> and Antarctic polar ice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">图示在地轴上标出两极，并比较北极和南极的极地冰层。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">Arctic ice</span> · 北极冰</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rotation</a></span> · 自转</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ice%20cap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ice cap</a></span> · 冰盖</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-068">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-068" class="vocabulary-word-title">Antarctic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-068" aria-label="定位 Antarctic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-068" data-mark-label="Antarctic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Antarctic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="Antarctic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Antarctic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 Antarctic" title="在有道查询 Antarctic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-068</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 南极的；n. [the ~] 南极地区，南极洲</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The Earth's axis runs through each pole, linking the Arctic and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Antarctic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Antarctic</strong></a> polar regions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地轴穿过两极，连接北极与南极的极地区域。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tilted axis explains seasonal daylight around each pole in the Arctic and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Antarctic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Antarctic</strong></a> polar zones.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">倾斜的地轴解释了北极和南极各极点周围极地区域的季节性日照。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The diagram marks each pole on the axis and compares Arctic and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Antarctic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Antarctic</strong></a> polar ice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">图示在地轴上标出两极，并比较北极和南极的极地冰层。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">Antarctic research</span> · 南极研究</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rotation</a></span> · 自转</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ice%20cap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ice cap</a></span> · 冰盖</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-069">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-069" class="vocabulary-word-title">pole<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-069" aria-label="定位 pole">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-069" data-mark-label="pole" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pole：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pole：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pole&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pole" title="在有道查询 pole">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-069</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.竿，杆</p>
<p class="vocabulary-usage"><span class="vocabulary-label">用法提示</span> 地理语境中，pole 也指地球的极点，如 the North Pole（北极）。本组例句使用这一义项。</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The Earth's axis runs through each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pole&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pole</strong></a>, linking the Arctic and Antarctic polar regions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地轴穿过两极，连接北极与南极的极地区域。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tilted axis explains seasonal daylight around each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pole&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pole</strong></a> in the Arctic and Antarctic polar zones.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">倾斜的地轴解释了北极和南极各极点周围极地区域的季节性日照。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The diagram marks each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pole&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pole</strong></a> on the axis and compares Arctic and Antarctic polar ice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">图示在地轴上标出两极，并比较北极和南极的极地冰层。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the North Pole</span> · 北极</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rotation</a></span> · 自转</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ice%20cap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ice cap</a></span> · 冰盖</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-070">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-070" class="vocabulary-word-title">polar<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-070" aria-label="定位 polar">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-070" data-mark-label="polar" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="polar：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="polar：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=polar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 polar" title="在有道查询 polar">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-070</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 极地的，近极地的；对立的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The Earth's axis runs through each pole, linking the Arctic and Antarctic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=polar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>polar</strong></a> regions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地轴穿过两极，连接北极与南极的极地区域。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tilted axis explains seasonal daylight around each pole in the Arctic and Antarctic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=polar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>polar</strong></a> zones.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">倾斜的地轴解释了北极和南极各极点周围极地区域的季节性日照。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The diagram marks each pole on the axis and compares Arctic and Antarctic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=polar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>polar</strong></a> ice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">图示在地轴上标出两极，并比较北极和南极的极地冰层。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">polar regions</span> · 极地地区</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rotation</a></span> · 自转</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ice%20cap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ice cap</a></span> · 冰盖</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The diagram marks each pole on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=axis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>axis</strong></a> and compares Arctic and Antarctic polar ice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">图示在地轴上标出两极，并比较北极和南极的极地冰层。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 01.04｜从岩石到土壤 {#group-01-04}

**串联方式：** 观察矿石与岩石，继续追踪沉积、淤泥、黏土和泥土，组成一张地表材料图，再给这门研究命名 geology。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-025">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-025" class="vocabulary-word-title">pebble<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-025" aria-label="定位 pebble">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-025" data-mark-label="pebble" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pebble：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pebble：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pebble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pebble" title="在有道查询 pebble">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-025</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 鹅卵石</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A magnet attracted the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pebble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pebble</strong></a>, suggesting it contained an iron mineral found in ore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">磁铁吸住了鹅卵石，表明其中含有矿石中常见的铁矿物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students used a magnet to compare a river <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pebble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pebble</strong></a> with ore rich in a magnetic mineral.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生用磁铁比较河中鹅卵石与富含磁性矿物的矿石。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The mineral in this ore responds to a magnet, unlike the quartz <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pebble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pebble</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种矿石中的矿物会被磁铁吸引，而石英鹅卵石不会。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a smooth pebble</span> · 光滑鹅卵石</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnetism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">magnetism</a></span> · 磁性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rock%20sample&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rock sample</a></span> · 岩石样本</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>magnet</strong></a> attracted the pebble, suggesting it contained an iron mineral found in ore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">磁铁吸住了鹅卵石，表明其中含有矿石中常见的铁矿物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students used a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>magnet</strong></a> to compare a river pebble with ore rich in a magnetic mineral.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生用磁铁比较河中鹅卵石与富含磁性矿物的矿石。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The mineral in this ore responds to a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>magnet</strong></a>, unlike the quartz pebble.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种矿石中的矿物会被磁铁吸引，而石英鹅卵石不会。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-029">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-029" class="vocabulary-word-title">marble<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-029" aria-label="定位 marble">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-029" data-mark-label="marble" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="marble：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="marble：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 marble" title="在有道查询 marble">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-029</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 大理石；(玩具)弹球</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In geology class, we compared <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marble</strong></a> with granite and identified quartz within the granite.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质课上，我们比较大理石与花岗岩，并辨认花岗岩中的石英。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The geology museum displays polished <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marble</strong></a> beside granite containing large quartz crystals.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质博物馆将抛光大理石与含大颗粒石英晶体的花岗岩并列展示。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Understanding geology helps builders distinguish <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marble</strong></a> from quartz-rich granite.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质学知识帮助建筑商区分大理石与富含石英的花岗岩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">polished marble</span> · 抛光大理石</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=igneous&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">igneous</a></span> · 火成的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=metamorphic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">metamorphic</a></span> · 变质的</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-030">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-030" class="vocabulary-word-title">quartz<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-030" aria-label="定位 quartz">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-030" data-mark-label="quartz" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="quartz：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="quartz：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quartz&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 quartz" title="在有道查询 quartz">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-030</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 石英</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In geology class, we compared marble with granite and identified <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quartz&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quartz</strong></a> within the granite.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质课上，我们比较大理石与花岗岩，并辨认花岗岩中的石英。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The geology museum displays polished marble beside granite containing large <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quartz&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quartz</strong></a> crystals.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质博物馆将抛光大理石与含大颗粒石英晶体的花岗岩并列展示。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Understanding geology helps builders distinguish marble from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quartz&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quartz</strong></a>-rich granite.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质学知识帮助建筑商区分大理石与富含石英的花岗岩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a quartz crystal</span> · 石英晶体</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=igneous&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">igneous</a></span> · 火成的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=metamorphic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">metamorphic</a></span> · 变质的</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 花岗岩</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In geology class, we compared marble with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=granite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>granite</strong></a> and identified quartz within the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=granite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>granite</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质课上，我们比较大理石与花岗岩，并辨认花岗岩中的石英。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The geology museum displays polished marble beside <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=granite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>granite</strong></a> containing large quartz crystals.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质博物馆将抛光大理石与含大颗粒石英晶体的花岗岩并列展示。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Understanding geology helps builders distinguish marble from quartz-rich <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=granite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>granite</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质学知识帮助建筑商区分大理石与富含石英的花岗岩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a granite cliff</span> · 花岗岩峭壁</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-160">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-160" class="vocabulary-word-title">sediment<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-160" aria-label="定位 sediment">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-160" data-mark-label="sediment" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sediment：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sediment：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sediment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sediment" title="在有道查询 sediment">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-160</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 沉淀物；沉积物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Muddy water carried <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sediment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sediment</strong></a> containing silt, clay and dirt washed from the road.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">浑浊的水携带沉积物，包括粉沙、黏土和从路上冲下来的泥土。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The flood left dirt and muddy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sediment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sediment</strong></a>, with silt above a layer of clay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洪水留下泥土和泥状沉积物，粉沙覆在一层黏土上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists analysed muddy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sediment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sediment</strong></a> to separate fine clay and silt from coarser dirt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家分析泥状沉积物，将细黏土及粉沙与较粗泥土分开。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">river sediment</span> · 河流沉积物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=erosion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">erosion</a></span> · 侵蚀</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deposition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">deposition</a></span> · 沉积</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-161">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-161" class="vocabulary-word-title">silt<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-161" aria-label="定位 silt">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-161" data-mark-label="silt" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="silt：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="silt：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 silt" title="在有道查询 silt">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-161</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 淤泥，泥沙；v.(使)淤塞</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Muddy water carried sediment containing <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>silt</strong></a>, clay and dirt washed from the road.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">浑浊的水携带沉积物，包括粉沙、黏土和从路上冲下来的泥土。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The flood left dirt and muddy sediment, with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>silt</strong></a> above a layer of clay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洪水留下泥土和泥状沉积物，粉沙覆在一层黏土上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists analysed muddy sediment to separate fine clay and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>silt</strong></a> from coarser dirt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家分析泥状沉积物，将细黏土及粉沙与较粗泥土分开。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">fine silt</span> · 细粉沙</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=erosion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">erosion</a></span> · 侵蚀</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deposition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">deposition</a></span> · 沉积</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The flood left dirt and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=muddy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>muddy</strong></a> sediment, with silt above a layer of clay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洪水留下泥土和泥状沉积物，粉沙覆在一层黏土上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists analysed <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=muddy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>muddy</strong></a> sediment to separate fine clay and silt from coarser dirt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家分析泥状沉积物，将细黏土及粉沙与较粗泥土分开。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 地质学；地质状况</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=geology&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>geology</strong></a> class, we compared marble with granite and identified quartz within the granite.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质课上，我们比较大理石与花岗岩，并辨认花岗岩中的石英。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=geology&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>geology</strong></a> museum displays polished marble beside granite containing large quartz crystals.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质博物馆将抛光大理石与含大颗粒石英晶体的花岗岩并列展示。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Understanding <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=geology&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>geology</strong></a> helps builders distinguish marble from quartz-rich granite.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地质学知识帮助建筑商区分大理石与富含石英的花岗岩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">study geology</span> · 研究地质学</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=igneous&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">igneous</a></span> · 火成的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=metamorphic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">metamorphic</a></span> · 变质的</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 01.05｜观察陆地地貌 {#group-01-05}

**串联方式：** 由大陆走向半岛、平原、高原与山崖，最后找出绿洲和湿地所在的位置。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-053">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-053" class="vocabulary-word-title">terrain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-053" aria-label="定位 terrain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-053" data-mark-label="terrain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="terrain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="terrain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terrain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 terrain" title="在有道查询 terrain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-053</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 地形</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The continent contains varied <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terrain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terrain</strong></a>, from a flat coastal landscape to rugged mountains.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这片大陆地形多样，从平坦海岸地貌到崎岖山地皆有。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Satellite images reveal how <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terrain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terrain</strong></a> shapes the landscape across the continent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">卫星图像揭示了整个大陆上地形如何塑造景观。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Crossing the continent, travellers encounter unfamiliar <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terrain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terrain</strong></a> and a constantly changing landscape.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">穿越大陆时，旅行者会遇到陌生地形和不断变化的景观。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">rugged terrain</span> · 崎岖地形</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">landform</a></span> · 地貌</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=topography&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">topography</a></span> · 地形</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Satellite images reveal how terrain shapes the landscape across the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=continent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>continent</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">卫星图像揭示了整个大陆上地形如何塑造景观。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-056">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-056" class="vocabulary-word-title">cave<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-056" aria-label="定位 cave">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-056" data-mark-label="cave" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cave：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cave：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cave" title="在有道查询 cave">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-056</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 洞穴，山洞</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A glacier once filled the valley below the cliff and the entrance to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cave</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冰川曾填满悬崖与洞口下方的山谷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">From the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cave</strong></a>, we saw ice break from the glacier near the cliff.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们从洞穴中看到悬崖附近的冰川崩落冰块。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guide warned us to avoid the cliff and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cave</strong></a> beneath the unstable glacier.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导游警告我们避开不稳定冰川下的悬崖和洞穴。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a limestone cave</span> · 石灰岩洞穴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ice%20melt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ice melt</a></span> · 融冰</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rock%20face&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rock face</a></span> · 岩壁</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A glacier once filled the valley below the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cliff&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cliff</strong></a> and the entrance to the cave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冰川曾填满悬崖与洞口下方的山谷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">From the cave, we saw ice break from the glacier near the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cliff&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cliff</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们从洞穴中看到悬崖附近的冰川崩落冰块。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guide warned us to avoid the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cliff&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cliff</strong></a> and cave beneath the unstable glacier.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导游警告我们避开不稳定冰川下的悬崖和洞穴。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a steep cliff</span> · 陡峭悬崖</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=glacier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>glacier</strong></a> once filled the valley below the cliff and the entrance to the cave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冰川曾填满悬崖与洞口下方的山谷。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-059">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-059" class="vocabulary-word-title">swamp<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-059" aria-label="定位 swamp">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-059" data-mark-label="swamp" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="swamp：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="swamp：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swamp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 swamp" title="在有道查询 swamp">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-059</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 沼泽，湿地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The map marks a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swamp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swamp</strong></a> in the delta, a broad plain, a high plateau and an oasis.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地图标出了三角洲中的沼泽、宽阔平原、高原及绿洲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Water from the plateau crosses a plain, feeds an oasis and eventually reaches a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swamp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swamp</strong></a> in the delta.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">高原的水流过平原、滋养绿洲，最终到达三角洲的沼泽。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The expedition crossed a plateau and plain, stopped at an oasis and surveyed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swamp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swamp</strong></a> near the delta.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">考察队穿越高原和平原，在绿洲休息，并调查三角洲附近的沼泽。</p></li>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 大洋洲</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The map of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Oceania&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Oceania</strong></a> shows a peninsula projecting from mainland Australia.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大洋洲地图显示了从澳大利亚大陆伸出的一个半岛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Travellers exploring <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Oceania&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Oceania</strong></a> reached the peninsula by road from the mainland.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">探索大洋洲的旅行者从大陆经公路抵达半岛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A study of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Oceania&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Oceania</strong></a> compared coastal erosion on the peninsula with conditions elsewhere on the mainland.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一项大洋洲研究将该半岛的海岸侵蚀与大陆其他地区的情况比较。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">islands of Oceania</span> · 大洋洲岛屿</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The map of Oceania shows a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peninsula&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peninsula</strong></a> projecting from mainland Australia.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大洋洲地图显示了从澳大利亚大陆伸出的一个半岛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Travellers exploring Oceania reached the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peninsula&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peninsula</strong></a> by road from the mainland.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">探索大洋洲的旅行者从大陆经公路抵达半岛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A study of Oceania compared coastal erosion on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peninsula&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peninsula</strong></a> with conditions elsewhere on the mainland.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一项大洋洲研究将该半岛的海岸侵蚀与大陆其他地区的情况比较。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a narrow peninsula</span> · 狭长半岛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=archipelago&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">archipelago</a></span> · 群岛</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=isthmus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">isthmus</a></span> · 地峡</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 01.06｜走进山地 {#group-01-06}

**串联方式：** 从山峰、山脉沿山脊和斜坡走向谷地，想象站在山坡上俯瞰。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-139">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-139" class="vocabulary-word-title">peak<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-139" aria-label="定位 peak">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-139" data-mark-label="peak" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="peak：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="peak：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 peak" title="在有道查询 peak">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-139</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 山峰；顶点；v. 达到最大值</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They plan to mount an expedition to the highest <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peak</strong></a> in this mountain range.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们计划组织考察，前往这条山脉的最高峰。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The team will mount another attempt to reach the mountain <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peak</strong></a> at the western end of the range.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">队伍将再次组织尝试，登上山脉西端的山峰。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we mount a rescue, locate the climbers below the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peak</strong></a> of the tallest mountain in the range.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">组织救援前，先确定登山者在山脉最高山峰下方的位置。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Paths on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ridge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ridge</strong></a> and hillside overlook a valley below the steep slope.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">山脊和山腰的小径俯瞰陡坡下的山谷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We climbed the hillside slope to reach a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ridge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ridge</strong></a> and overlook the valley.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们攀上山腰斜坡到达山脊，俯瞰山谷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Houses on the hillside overlook the valley, while a narrow <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ridge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ridge</strong></a> rises above the opposite slope.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">山腰房屋俯瞰山谷，一道狭窄山脊则耸立于对面的斜坡上方。</p></li>
