<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Paths on the ridge and hillside overlook a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=valley&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>valley</strong></a> below the steep slope.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">山脊和山腰的小径俯瞰陡坡下的山谷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We climbed the hillside slope to reach a ridge and overlook the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=valley&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>valley</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们攀上山腰斜坡到达山脊，俯瞰山谷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Houses on the hillside overlook the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=valley&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>valley</strong></a>, while a narrow ridge rises above the opposite slope.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">山腰房屋俯瞰山谷，一道狭窄山脊则耸立于对面的斜坡上方。</p></li>

### 词群 01.07｜从海洋回到水源 {#group-01-07}

**串联方式：** 先看大洋与海湾，再看海岸、潮汐和洋流，沿溪流回溯到水源及蓄水处。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-077">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-077" class="vocabulary-word-title">Mediterranean<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-077" aria-label="定位 Mediterranean">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-077" data-mark-label="Mediterranean" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Mediterranean：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="Mediterranean：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Mediterranean&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 Mediterranean" title="在有道查询 Mediterranean">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-077</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 地中海的；地中海地区的；n. 地中海；地中海地区</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The atlas compares the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Mediterranean&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Mediterranean</strong></a> with the Atlantic and Pacific ocean basins.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地图集比较地中海与大西洋、太平洋海盆。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Mediterranean&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Mediterranean</strong></a>, the Atlantic and Pacific each form a vast ocean.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与地中海不同，大西洋和太平洋各自构成广阔的大洋。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The vessel crossed the Atlantic, entered the Pacific ocean basin and later returned to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Mediterranean&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Mediterranean</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船横渡大西洋、进入太平洋海盆，之后返回地中海。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">Mediterranean waters</span> · 地中海水域</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strait&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">strait</a></span> · 海峡</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=basin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">basin</a></span> · 海盆</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-078">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-078" class="vocabulary-word-title">Atlantic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-078" aria-label="定位 Atlantic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-078" data-mark-label="Atlantic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Atlantic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="Atlantic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Atlantic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 Atlantic" title="在有道查询 Atlantic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-078</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 大西洋的；n. [the ~] 大西洋</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The atlas compares the Mediterranean with the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Atlantic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Atlantic</strong></a> and Pacific ocean basins.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地图集比较地中海与大西洋、太平洋海盆。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the Mediterranean, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Atlantic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Atlantic</strong></a> and Pacific each form a vast ocean.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与地中海不同，大西洋和太平洋各自构成广阔的大洋。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The vessel crossed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Atlantic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Atlantic</strong></a>, entered the Pacific ocean basin and later returned to the Mediterranean.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船横渡大西洋、进入太平洋海盆，之后返回地中海。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the Atlantic Ocean</span> · 大西洋</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strait&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">strait</a></span> · 海峡</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=basin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">basin</a></span> · 海盆</li></ul>

</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the Mediterranean, the Atlantic and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pacific&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Pacific</strong></a> each form a vast ocean.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与地中海不同，大西洋和太平洋各自构成广阔的大洋。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The vessel crossed the Atlantic, entered the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pacific&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Pacific</strong></a> ocean basin and later returned to the Mediterranean.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船横渡大西洋、进入太平洋海盆，之后返回地中海。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 海生的，海洋的；海事的；n. 水兵</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Safe navigation through the gulf requires charts that also identify protected <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marine</strong></a> habitats.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">安全穿越海湾需要海图，其中还须标出受保护的海洋栖息地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Marine</strong></a> pollution in the gulf increased after a navigation error caused an oil spill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次航行失误导致漏油后，海湾的海洋污染加重。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">New navigation rules protect <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marine</strong></a> life while allowing vessels to enter the gulf.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新的航行规定保护海洋生物，同时允许船舶进入海湾。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">marine life</span> · 海洋生物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shipping&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shipping</a></span> · 航运</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=estuary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">estuary</a></span> · 河口</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Marine pollution in the gulf increased after a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=navigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>navigation</strong></a> error caused an oil spill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次航行失误导致漏油后，海湾的海洋污染加重。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">New <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=navigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>navigation</strong></a> rules protect marine life while allowing vessels to enter the gulf.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新的航行规定保护海洋生物，同时允许船舶进入海湾。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Marine pollution in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gulf&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gulf</strong></a> increased after a navigation error caused an oil spill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次航行失误导致漏油后，海湾的海洋污染加重。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-084">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-084" class="vocabulary-word-title">beach<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-084" aria-label="定位 beach">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-084" data-mark-label="beach" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="beach：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="beach：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=beach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 beach" title="在有道查询 beach">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-084</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 海滩，河滩</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong current and rising tide made the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=beach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>beach</strong></a> unsafe along this part of the coast and shore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强水流和涨潮使这段沿海岸边的沙滩变得不安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tide carried driftwood along the coast, while the current pushed it towards the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=beach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>beach</strong></a> and shore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">潮水沿海岸带动浮木，水流则将其推向沙滩和岸边。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We left the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=beach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>beach</strong></a> as the tide rose and watched a current sweep along the rocky coast near the shore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">涨潮时我们离开沙滩，看着水流沿岸边的岩石海岸奔流。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sandy beach</span> · 沙滩</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rip%20current&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rip current</a></span> · 离岸流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coastline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coastline</a></span> · 海岸线</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-085">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 海岸，海滨</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong current and rising tide made the beach unsafe along this part of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coast</strong></a> and shore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强水流和涨潮使这段沿海岸边的沙滩变得不安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tide carried driftwood along the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coast</strong></a>, while the current pushed it towards the beach and shore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">潮水沿海岸带动浮木，水流则将其推向沙滩和岸边。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We left the beach as the tide rose and watched a current sweep along the rocky <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coast</strong></a> near the shore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">涨潮时我们离开沙滩，看着水流沿岸边的岩石海岸奔流。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a rocky coast</span> · 岩石海岸</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We left the beach as the tide rose and watched a current sweep along the rocky coast near the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shore</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">涨潮时我们离开沙滩，看着水流沿岸边的岩石海岸奔流。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tide</strong></a> carried driftwood along the coast, while the current pushed it towards the beach and shore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">潮水沿海岸带动浮木，水流则将其推向沙滩和岸边。</p></li>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 水流；洋流；潮流；adj. 当前的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=current&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>current</strong></a> and rising tide made the beach unsafe along this part of the coast and shore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强水流和涨潮使这段沿海岸边的沙滩变得不安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tide carried driftwood along the coast, while the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=current&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>current</strong></a> pushed it towards the beach and shore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">潮水沿海岸带动浮木，水流则将其推向沙滩和岸边。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We left the beach as the tide rose and watched a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=current&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>current</strong></a> sweep along the rocky coast near the shore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">涨潮时我们离开沙滩，看着水流沿岸边的岩石海岸奔流。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a strong current</span> · 强水流</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rip%20current&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rip current</a></span> · 离岸流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coastline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coastline</a></span> · 海岸线</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-089">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-089" class="vocabulary-word-title">brook<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-089" aria-label="定位 brook">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-089" data-mark-label="brook" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="brook：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="brook：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 brook" title="在有道查询 brook">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-089</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 小河，溪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brook</strong></a> joins a larger stream near the river's source.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小溪在河流源头附近汇入一条较大的溪流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We followed the stream uphill until a narrow <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brook</strong></a> led us to its source.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们沿溪流上行，最后一条窄小溪流引我们找到其源头。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Pollution near the source spread from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brook</strong></a> into the main stream.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">源头附近的污染从小溪扩散到主溪流。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an upland brook</span> · 高地溪流</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tributary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tributary</a></span> · 支流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=headwater&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">headwater</a></span> · 源流</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-090">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-090" class="vocabulary-word-title">stream<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-090" aria-label="定位 stream">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-090" data-mark-label="stream" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="stream：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="stream：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stream&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 stream" title="在有道查询 stream">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-090</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 小河，溪；流；v. 流动，流出</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The brook joins a larger <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stream&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stream</strong></a> near the river's source.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小溪在河流源头附近汇入一条较大的溪流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We followed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stream&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stream</strong></a> uphill until a narrow brook led us to its source.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们沿溪流上行，最后一条窄小溪流引我们找到其源头。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Pollution near the source spread from the brook into the main <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stream&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stream</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">源头附近的污染从小溪扩散到主溪流。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a mountain stream</span> · 山溪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tributary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tributary</a></span> · 支流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=headwater&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">headwater</a></span> · 源流</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We followed the stream uphill until a narrow brook led us to its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=source&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>source</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们沿溪流上行，最后一条窄小溪流引我们找到其源头。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Pollution near the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=source&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>source</strong></a> spread from the brook into the main stream.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">源头附近的污染从小溪扩散到主溪流。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-212">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-212" class="vocabulary-word-title">reservoir<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-212" aria-label="定位 reservoir">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-212" data-mark-label="reservoir" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reservoir：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reservoir：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reservoir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reservoir" title="在有道查询 reservoir">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-212</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 水库，蓄水池</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Water from a spring feeds the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reservoir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reservoir</strong></a>, then flows past a waterfall to a village fountain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">泉水注入水库，再流经瀑布，抵达村中喷泉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The path passes a spring, a waterfall and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reservoir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reservoir</strong></a> before ending at a fountain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小径经过泉水、瀑布和水库，最终到达喷泉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers traced water from the village fountain back through the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reservoir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reservoir</strong></a> to a spring above the waterfall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师从村中喷泉沿水库追溯水源，找到瀑布上方的一处泉眼。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a water reservoir</span> · 水库</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catchment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">catchment</a></span> · 集水区</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=groundwater&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">groundwater</a></span> · 地下水</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-213">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-213" class="vocabulary-word-title">waterfall<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-213" aria-label="定位 waterfall">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-213" data-mark-label="waterfall" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="waterfall：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="waterfall：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waterfall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 waterfall" title="在有道查询 waterfall">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-213</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 瀑布</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Water from a spring feeds the reservoir, then flows past a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waterfall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>waterfall</strong></a> to a village fountain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">泉水注入水库，再流经瀑布，抵达村中喷泉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The path passes a spring, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waterfall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>waterfall</strong></a> and the reservoir before ending at a fountain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小径经过泉水、瀑布和水库，最终到达喷泉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers traced water from the village fountain back through the reservoir to a spring above the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waterfall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>waterfall</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师从村中喷泉沿水库追溯水源，找到瀑布上方的一处泉眼。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a spectacular waterfall</span> · 壮观瀑布</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catchment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">catchment</a></span> · 集水区</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=groundwater&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">groundwater</a></span> · 地下水</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Water from a spring feeds the reservoir, then flows past a waterfall to a village <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fountain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fountain</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">泉水注入水库，再流经瀑布，抵达村中喷泉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The path passes a spring, a waterfall and the reservoir before ending at a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fountain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fountain</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小径经过泉水、瀑布和水库，最终到达喷泉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers traced water from the village <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fountain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fountain</strong></a> back through the reservoir to a spring above the waterfall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师从村中喷泉沿水库追溯水源，找到瀑布上方的一处泉眼。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

### 词群 01.08｜描述地表的形状与质地 {#group-01-08}

**串联方式：** 同一处地貌可以从深浅、平滑粗糙、沙石构成、坡度和宽窄来描述；superficial 另记引申义“肤浅的”。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-092">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-092" class="vocabulary-word-title">shallow<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-092" aria-label="定位 shallow">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-092" data-mark-label="shallow" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="shallow：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="shallow：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shallow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 shallow" title="在有道查询 shallow">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-092</span></span></summary>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The builder found superficial wear on a smooth, flat tile beneath the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shallow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shallow</strong></a> water.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">建筑工人在浅水下的一块光滑平坦瓷砖上发现表面磨损。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Two parallel paths climb the steep slope beside a narrow gorge with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vertical&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vertical</strong></a> walls.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">两条平行小径沿陡坡上升，旁边是有垂直岩壁的狭窄峡谷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The narrow staircase is steep, with parallel rails fixed to a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vertical&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vertical</strong></a> wall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">狭窄楼梯很陡，平行扶手固定于垂直墙面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We avoided the steep, narrow route and followed a parallel path below the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vertical&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vertical</strong></a> cliff.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们避开陡峭狭窄的路线，沿垂直悬崖下的一条平行小路行进。</p></li>

### 词群 01.09｜描述气候与温度 {#group-01-09}

**串联方式：** 区分长期气候和当天气象，再从温度、干湿程度进入气象预报。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-106">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-106" class="vocabulary-word-title">climate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-106" aria-label="定位 climate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-106" data-mark-label="climate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="climate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="climate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=climate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 climate" title="在有道查询 climate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-106</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 气候；风气，思潮，环境</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Meteorology studies weather, while long-term observations help us understand <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=climate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>climate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气象学研究天气，长期观测则帮助我们理解气候。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Advances in meteorology improve weather forecasts and provide data for <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=climate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>climate</strong></a> research.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气象学进展改善天气预报，并为气候研究提供数据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students of meteorology distinguish today's weather from the region's long-term <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=climate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>climate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气象学学生区分今日天气与该地区长期气候。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">climate change</span> · 气候变化</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=climatology&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">climatology</a></span> · 气候学</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forecasting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">forecasting</a></span> · 天气预测</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-107">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-107" class="vocabulary-word-title">weather<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-107" aria-label="定位 weather">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-107" data-mark-label="weather" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="weather：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="weather：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=weather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 weather" title="在有道查询 weather">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-107</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 天气，气象</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Meteorology studies <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=weather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>weather</strong></a>, while long-term observations help us understand climate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气象学研究天气，长期观测则帮助我们理解气候。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Advances in meteorology improve <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=weather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>weather</strong></a> forecasts and provide data for climate research.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气象学进展改善天气预报，并为气候研究提供数据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students of meteorology distinguish today's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=weather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>weather</strong></a> from the region's long-term climate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气象学学生区分今日天气与该地区长期气候。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">extreme weather</span> · 极端天气</p>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mild&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Mild</strong></a> weather and good thermal insulation keep rooms warm with moderate heating.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">温和天气和良好保温使房间只需适度供暖就能保持温暖。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Thermal curtains reduce heating needs and keep a warm room at a moderate temperature during a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mild&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mild</strong></a> winter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">保温窗帘减少供暖需求，在温和冬天让温暖房间保持适中温度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mild&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mild</strong></a> climate permits moderate heating, while thermal mass in walls keeps the room warm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">温和气候使适度供暖成为可能，墙体的蓄热能力则让房间保持温暖。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A mild climate permits <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=moderate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>moderate</strong></a> heating, while thermal mass in walls keeps the room warm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">温和气候使适度供暖成为可能，墙体的蓄热能力则让房间保持温暖。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-114">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-114" class="vocabulary-word-title">tropics<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-114" aria-label="定位 tropics">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-114" data-mark-label="tropics" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tropics：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tropics：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tropics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tropics" title="在有道查询 tropics">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-114</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [the ~] 热带地区</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Even in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tropics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tropics</strong></a>, arid areas contrast with humid forests where moist soil keeps walls damp.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使在热带，干旱地区也与空气潮湿、湿润土壤使墙面受潮的森林形成对照。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike an arid interior, this part of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tropics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tropics</strong></a> has humid air, moist ground and damp buildings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与干旱内陆不同，热带这一地区空气湿润、地面湿润、建筑潮湿。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit contrasts arid deserts with humid parts of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tropics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tropics</strong></a>, showing moist soil and damp forest floors.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将干旱沙漠与热带湿润地区比较，展示湿润土壤及潮湿林地表面。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">in the tropics</span> · 在热带</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=humidity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">humidity</a></span> · 湿度</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=moisture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">moisture</a></span> · 水分</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Even in the tropics, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arid</strong></a> areas contrast with humid forests where moist soil keeps walls damp.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使在热带，干旱地区也与空气潮湿、湿润土壤使墙面受潮的森林形成对照。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arid</strong></a> interior, this part of the tropics has humid air, moist ground and damp buildings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与干旱内陆不同，热带这一地区空气湿润、地面湿润、建筑潮湿。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit contrasts <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arid</strong></a> deserts with humid parts of the tropics, showing moist soil and damp forest floors.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将干旱沙漠与热带湿润地区比较，展示湿润土壤及潮湿林地表面。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-116">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-116" class="vocabulary-word-title">moist<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-116" aria-label="定位 moist">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-116" data-mark-label="moist" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="moist：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="moist：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=moist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 moist" title="在有道查询 moist">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-116</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 潮湿的，湿润的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Even in the tropics, arid areas contrast with humid forests where <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=moist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>moist</strong></a> soil keeps walls damp.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使在热带，干旱地区也与空气潮湿、湿润土壤使墙面受潮的森林形成对照。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike an arid interior, this part of the tropics has humid air, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=moist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>moist</strong></a> ground and damp buildings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与干旱内陆不同，热带这一地区空气湿润、地面湿润、建筑潮湿。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit contrasts arid deserts with humid parts of the tropics, showing <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=moist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>moist</strong></a> soil and damp forest floors.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将干旱沙漠与热带湿润地区比较，展示湿润土壤及潮湿林地表面。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">moist soil</span> · 湿润土壤</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=humidity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">humidity</a></span> · 湿度</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=moisture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">moisture</a></span> · 水分</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-136">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-136" class="vocabulary-word-title">Celsius<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-136" aria-label="定位 Celsius">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-136" data-mark-label="Celsius" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Celsius：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="Celsius：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Celsius&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 Celsius" title="在有道查询 Celsius">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-136</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 摄氏的；n. 摄氏温度</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The forecast predicts a temperature of thirty degrees <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Celsius&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Celsius</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">预报预计气温为三十摄氏度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A temperature below zero degrees <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Celsius&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Celsius</strong></a> appeared in the overnight forecast.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">夜间预报显示气温将降至零摄氏度以下。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The forecast was revised after the temperature rose five degrees <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Celsius&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Celsius</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气温上升五摄氏度后，预报作了修订。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">degrees Celsius</span> · 摄氏度</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thermometer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">thermometer</a></span> · 温度计</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Fahrenheit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fahrenheit</a></span> · 华氏温标</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-137">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-137" class="vocabulary-word-title">temperature<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-137" aria-label="定位 temperature">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-137" data-mark-label="temperature" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="temperature：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="temperature：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=temperature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 temperature" title="在有道查询 temperature">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-137</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 气温；体温；温度</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The forecast predicts a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=temperature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>temperature</strong></a> of thirty degrees Celsius.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">预报预计气温为三十摄氏度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=temperature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>temperature</strong></a> below zero degrees Celsius appeared in the overnight forecast.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">夜间预报显示气温将降至零摄氏度以下。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The forecast was revised after the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=temperature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>temperature</strong></a> rose five degrees Celsius.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气温上升五摄氏度后，预报作了修订。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a rising temperature</span> · 上升的温度</p>

</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forecast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>forecast</strong></a> predicts a temperature of thirty degrees Celsius.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">预报预计气温为三十摄氏度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A temperature below zero degrees Celsius appeared in the overnight <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forecast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>forecast</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">夜间预报显示气温将降至零摄氏度以下。</p></li>

### 词群 01.10｜观察风雨 {#group-01-10}

**串联方式：** 从微风到强风辨认强度，再把雷电、暴雨、小雨与雨后彩虹放到天气场景中。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-032">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-032" class="vocabulary-word-title">gust<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-032" aria-label="定位 gust">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-032" data-mark-label="gust" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="gust：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="gust：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 gust" title="在有道查询 gust">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-032</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 一阵狂风；(感情的)迸发</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the monsoon, a gentle breeze became a gale, with each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gust</strong></a> shaking the windows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风期间，微风变成大风，一阵阵强风震动窗户。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A forecast for the monsoon warned that a breeze could strengthen into a gale with a powerful <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gust</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风预报警告，微风可能增强为大风，并出现强烈阵风。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the monsoon gale passed, only an occasional <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gust</strong></a> interrupted the soft breeze.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风带来的大风过去后，只有偶尔的阵风打断柔和微风。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sudden gust</span> · 突来阵风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wind%20speed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wind speed</a></span> · 风速</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=air%20pressure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">air pressure</a></span> · 气压</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-033">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-033" class="vocabulary-word-title">breeze<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-033" aria-label="定位 breeze">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-033" data-mark-label="breeze" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="breeze：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="breeze：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breeze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 breeze" title="在有道查询 breeze">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-033</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 微风，和风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the monsoon, a gentle <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breeze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>breeze</strong></a> became a gale, with each gust shaking the windows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风期间，微风变成大风，一阵阵强风震动窗户。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A forecast for the monsoon warned that a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breeze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>breeze</strong></a> could strengthen into a gale with a powerful gust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风预报警告，微风可能增强为大风，并出现强烈阵风。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the monsoon gale passed, only an occasional gust interrupted the soft <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breeze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>breeze</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风带来的大风过去后，只有偶尔的阵风打断柔和微风。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a gentle breeze</span> · 微风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wind%20speed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wind speed</a></span> · 风速</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=air%20pressure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">air pressure</a></span> · 气压</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-034">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-034" class="vocabulary-word-title">monsoon<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-034" aria-label="定位 monsoon">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-034" data-mark-label="monsoon" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="monsoon：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="monsoon：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monsoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 monsoon" title="在有道查询 monsoon">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-034</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 季风；雨季</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monsoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>monsoon</strong></a>, a gentle breeze became a gale, with each gust shaking the windows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风期间，微风变成大风，一阵阵强风震动窗户。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A forecast for the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monsoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>monsoon</strong></a> warned that a breeze could strengthen into a gale with a powerful gust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风预报警告，微风可能增强为大风，并出现强烈阵风。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monsoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>monsoon</strong></a> gale passed, only an occasional gust interrupted the soft breeze.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风带来的大风过去后，只有偶尔的阵风打断柔和微风。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the summer monsoon</span> · 夏季季风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wind%20speed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wind speed</a></span> · 风速</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=air%20pressure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">air pressure</a></span> · 气压</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-035">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-035" class="vocabulary-word-title">gale<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-035" aria-label="定位 gale">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-035" data-mark-label="gale" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="gale：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="gale：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 gale" title="在有道查询 gale">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-035</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 大风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the monsoon, a gentle breeze became a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gale</strong></a>, with each gust shaking the windows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风期间，微风变成大风，一阵阵强风震动窗户。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A forecast for the monsoon warned that a breeze could strengthen into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gale</strong></a> with a powerful gust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风预报警告，微风可能增强为大风，并出现强烈阵风。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the monsoon <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gale</strong></a> passed, only an occasional gust interrupted the soft breeze.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季风带来的大风过去后，只有偶尔的阵风打断柔和微风。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a fierce gale</span> · 强劲大风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wind%20speed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wind speed</a></span> · 风速</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=air%20pressure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">air pressure</a></span> · 气压</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-036">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-036" class="vocabulary-word-title">hurricane<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-036" aria-label="定位 hurricane">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-036" data-mark-label="hurricane" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hurricane：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hurricane：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurricane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hurricane" title="在有道查询 hurricane">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-036</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 飓风；暴风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurricane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hurricane</strong></a> and a typhoon are regional names for tropical cyclones, unlike a tornado.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飓风和台风是热带气旋的地区名称，与龙卷风不同。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The training explains shelter procedures for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurricane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hurricane</strong></a>, a tornado and a typhoon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">培训讲解飓风、龙卷风和台风的避险程序。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurricane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hurricane</strong></a> or typhoon may affect a vast area, while a tornado usually follows a narrower path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飓风或台风可能影响广阔地区，龙卷风通常沿较窄路径移动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a powerful hurricane</span> · 强飓风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cyclone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cyclone</a></span> · 气旋</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=storm%20surge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">storm surge</a></span> · 风暴潮</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-037">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-037" class="vocabulary-word-title">tornado<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-037" aria-label="定位 tornado">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-037" data-mark-label="tornado" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tornado：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tornado：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tornado&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tornado" title="在有道查询 tornado">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-037</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 龙卷风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A hurricane and a typhoon are regional names for tropical cyclones, unlike a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tornado&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tornado</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飓风和台风是热带气旋的地区名称，与龙卷风不同。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The training explains shelter procedures for a hurricane, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tornado&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tornado</strong></a> and a typhoon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">培训讲解飓风、龙卷风和台风的避险程序。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A hurricane or typhoon may affect a vast area, while a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tornado&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tornado</strong></a> usually follows a narrower path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飓风或台风可能影响广阔地区，龙卷风通常沿较窄路径移动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a destructive tornado</span> · 破坏性龙卷风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cyclone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cyclone</a></span> · 气旋</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=storm%20surge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">storm surge</a></span> · 风暴潮</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-038">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-038" class="vocabulary-word-title">typhoon<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-038" aria-label="定位 typhoon">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-038" data-mark-label="typhoon" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="typhoon：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="typhoon：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=typhoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 typhoon" title="在有道查询 typhoon">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-038</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 台风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A hurricane and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=typhoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>typhoon</strong></a> are regional names for tropical cyclones, unlike a tornado.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飓风和台风是热带气旋的地区名称，与龙卷风不同。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The training explains shelter procedures for a hurricane, a tornado and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=typhoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>typhoon</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">培训讲解飓风、龙卷风和台风的避险程序。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A hurricane or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=typhoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>typhoon</strong></a> may affect a vast area, while a tornado usually follows a narrower path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飓风或台风可能影响广阔地区，龙卷风通常沿较窄路径移动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an approaching typhoon</span> · 逼近的台风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cyclone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cyclone</a></span> · 气旋</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=storm%20surge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">storm surge</a></span> · 风暴潮</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-128">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-128" class="vocabulary-word-title">thunder<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-128" aria-label="定位 thunder">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-128" data-mark-label="thunder" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="thunder：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="thunder：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thunder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 thunder" title="在有道查询 thunder">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-128</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 雷；雷声；v. 打雷；轰隆隆地快速移动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Lightning flashed and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thunder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>thunder</strong></a> rolled as stormy weather brought a sudden downpour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">闪电划过、雷声隆隆，暴风雨天气带来突降暴雨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the stormy afternoon, a downpour arrived with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thunder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>thunder</strong></a> and lightning.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">暴风雨频发的下午，暴雨伴着雷电到来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thunder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Thunder</strong></a> and lightning forced us indoors before a downpour made the evening even more stormy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雷电迫使我们进屋，随后暴雨使夜晚风雨更甚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">rolling thunder</span> · 隆隆雷声</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thunderstorm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">thunderstorm</a></span> · 雷暴</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shelter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shelter</a></span> · 避雨处</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-129">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-129" class="vocabulary-word-title">lightning<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-129" aria-label="定位 lightning">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-129" data-mark-label="lightning" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="lightning：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="lightning：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lightning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 lightning" title="在有道查询 lightning">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-129</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 闪电；adj. 闪电般的，极快的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lightning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Lightning</strong></a> flashed and thunder rolled as stormy weather brought a sudden downpour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">闪电划过、雷声隆隆，暴风雨天气带来突降暴雨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the stormy afternoon, a downpour arrived with thunder and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lightning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lightning</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">暴风雨频发的下午，暴雨伴着雷电到来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Thunder and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lightning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lightning</strong></a> forced us indoors before a downpour made the evening even more stormy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雷电迫使我们进屋，随后暴雨使夜晚风雨更甚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a lightning strike</span> · 一次雷击</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thunderstorm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">thunderstorm</a></span> · 雷暴</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shelter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shelter</a></span> · 避雨处</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-130">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stormy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stormy</strong></a> afternoon, a downpour arrived with thunder and lightning.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">暴风雨频发的下午，暴雨伴着雷电到来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Thunder and lightning forced us indoors before a downpour made the evening even more <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stormy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stormy</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雷电迫使我们进屋，随后暴雨使夜晚风雨更甚。</p></li>
</ol>
