<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-132">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-132" class="vocabulary-word-title">rainfall<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-132" aria-label="定位 rainfall">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-132" data-mark-label="rainfall" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rainfall：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rainfall：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rainfall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rainfall" title="在有道查询 rainfall">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-132</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 降雨量</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A light sprinkle became a shower, adding to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rainfall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rainfall</strong></a> before sunshine revealed a rainbow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">零星小雨变成阵雨，增加了降雨量，随后阳光映出彩虹。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rainfall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rainfall</strong></a> record counted the brief shower, though a sprinkle continued beneath the rainbow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">降雨记录计入了那场短阵雨，尽管彩虹下仍飘着零星小雨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After days without <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rainfall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rainfall</strong></a>, a shower brought a rainbow and enough water to sprinkle the seedlings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">多日无雨后，一场阵雨带来彩虹，也带来足够给幼苗洒浇的水。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">annual rainfall</span> · 年降雨量</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=precipitation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">precipitation</a></span> · 降水</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunlight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sunlight</a></span> · 阳光</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-133">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-133" class="vocabulary-word-title">sprinkle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-133" aria-label="定位 sprinkle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-133" data-mark-label="sprinkle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sprinkle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sprinkle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sprinkle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sprinkle" title="在有道查询 sprinkle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-133</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 撒，洒；下小雨；n. 少量；小雨</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A light <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sprinkle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sprinkle</strong></a> became a shower, adding to the rainfall before sunshine revealed a rainbow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">零星小雨变成阵雨，增加了降雨量，随后阳光映出彩虹。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rainfall record counted the brief shower, though a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sprinkle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sprinkle</strong></a> continued beneath the rainbow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">降雨记录计入了那场短阵雨，尽管彩虹下仍飘着零星小雨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After days without rainfall, a shower brought a rainbow and enough water to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sprinkle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sprinkle</strong></a> the seedlings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">多日无雨后，一场阵雨带来彩虹，也带来足够给幼苗洒浇的水。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sprinkle water</span> · 洒水</p>

</div>
</details>

### 词群 01.11｜温度下降后的变化 {#group-01-11}

**串联方式：** 从降雪、霜雹到结冰，再看融化；用寒冷引起的颤抖连接身体体验。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-119">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-119" class="vocabulary-word-title">snowy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-119" aria-label="定位 snowy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-119" data-mark-label="snowy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="snowy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="snowy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=snowy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 snowy" title="在有道查询 snowy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-119</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 下雪多的；被雪覆盖的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=snowy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>snowy</strong></a> morning followed a night of hail and severe frost.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">下雪的早晨之前，夜里下了冰雹并出现严霜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Farmers protected crops from frost after hail damaged leaves during the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=snowy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>snowy</strong></a> week.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在下雪的一周里冰雹损伤叶片后，农民保护作物免受霜冻。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The forecast warned of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=snowy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>snowy</strong></a> roads, overnight frost and possible hail.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">预报警告道路可能积雪、夜间有霜冻，也可能下冰雹。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">snowy weather</span> · 下雪天气</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-122">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-122" class="vocabulary-word-title">thaw<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-122" aria-label="定位 thaw">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-122" data-mark-label="thaw" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="thaw：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="thaw：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thaw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 thaw" title="在有道查询 thaw">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-122</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 解冻，融解，融化；n. 解冻时期</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Frigid air can freeze pipes before a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thaw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>thaw</strong></a> ends the winter chill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">严寒空气可能在解冻消除冬日寒意之前冻住水管。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lake will freeze in this frigid chill and remain icy until the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thaw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>thaw</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">湖泊会在这种严寒中结冰，并一直冻结到解冻时节。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A brief <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thaw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>thaw</strong></a> gave way to frigid conditions, causing puddles to freeze again in the chill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">短暂解冻后转为严寒，寒气使水洼再次冻结。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">spring thaw</span> · 春季解冻</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=frozen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">frozen</a></span> · 冻结的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defrost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defrost</a></span> · 解冻</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-123">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-123" class="vocabulary-word-title">chill<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-123" aria-label="定位 chill">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-123" data-mark-label="chill" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="chill：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="chill：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 chill" title="在有道查询 chill">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-123</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(使)变冷；使恐惧；n. 寒冷；害怕</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Frigid air can freeze pipes before a thaw ends the winter <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chill</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">严寒空气可能在解冻消除冬日寒意之前冻住水管。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lake will freeze in this frigid <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chill</strong></a> and remain icy until the thaw.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">湖泊会在这种严寒中结冰，并一直冻结到解冻时节。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A brief thaw gave way to frigid conditions, causing puddles to freeze again in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chill</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">短暂解冻后转为严寒，寒气使水洼再次冻结。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a winter chill</span> · 冬日寒意</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=frozen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">frozen</a></span> · 冻结的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defrost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defrost</a></span> · 解冻</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lake will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=freeze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>freeze</strong></a> in this frigid chill and remain icy until the thaw.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">湖泊会在这种严寒中结冰，并一直冻结到解冻时节。</p></li>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.战栗，颤抖；n. 战栗，颤抖</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Wet clothes made her shiver and her hands <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tremble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tremble</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">湿衣服使她打寒战，双手发抖。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We began to shiver as we watched the cold puppy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tremble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tremble</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看着受冻的小狗发抖时，也开始打寒战。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wind made the children shiver and their lips <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tremble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tremble</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">风使孩子们打寒战，嘴唇颤抖。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">tremble with cold</span> · 冷得发抖</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shivering&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shivering</a></span> · 颤抖</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hypothermia&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hypothermia</a></span> · 失温</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 01.12｜自然灾害如何发生 {#group-01-12}

**串联方式：** 用火山、水灾、干旱和地震举例，最后用灾难、危害及破坏程度作总结；温室与厄尔尼诺提供气候背景。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-015">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-015" class="vocabulary-word-title">disaster<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-015" aria-label="定位 disaster">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-015" data-mark-label="disaster" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="disaster：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="disaster：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disaster&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 disaster" title="在有道查询 disaster">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-015</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 灾难</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">What began as a minor mishap became a catastrophic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disaster&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disaster</strong></a> and a national calamity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">起初的小事故演变为灾难性事件，成为国家灾难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike a minor mishap, this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disaster&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disaster</strong></a> caused catastrophic losses and was remembered as a calamity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与小事故不同，这场灾害造成灾难性损失，被人们铭记为大灾难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The inquiry asked how a preventable mishap became a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disaster&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disaster</strong></a> with catastrophic consequences and widespread calamity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查追问，一个可预防的小事故为何演变为后果惨重、灾祸广泛的灾难。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a natural disaster</span> · 自然灾害</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catastrophe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">catastrophe</a></span> · 灾难</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emergency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">emergency</a></span> · 紧急情况</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-019">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-019" class="vocabulary-word-title">endanger<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-019" aria-label="定位 endanger">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-019" data-mark-label="endanger" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="endanger：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="endanger：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endanger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 endanger" title="在有道查询 endanger">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-019</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 使遭受危险，危及</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Destructive winds <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endanger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>endanger</strong></a> residents and jeopardise rescue operations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">破坏性强风危及居民，并威胁救援行动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Destructive mining practices can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endanger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>endanger</strong></a> wildlife and jeopardise local water supplies.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">破坏性采矿可能危及野生生物并威胁当地供水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Building on unstable slopes may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endanger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>endanger</strong></a> lives and jeopardise recovery after a destructive storm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在不稳定坡地建房可能危及生命，并损害破坏性风暴后的恢复工作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">endanger lives</span> · 危及生命</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=threaten&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">threaten</a></span> · 威胁</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=damage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">damage</a></span> · 损害</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-020">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-020" class="vocabulary-word-title">jeopardise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-020" aria-label="定位 jeopardise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-020" data-mark-label="jeopardise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="jeopardise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="jeopardise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jeopardise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 jeopardise" title="在有道查询 jeopardise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-020</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 危害，危及</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Destructive winds endanger residents and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jeopardise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jeopardise</strong></a> rescue operations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">破坏性强风危及居民，并威胁救援行动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Destructive mining practices can endanger wildlife and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jeopardise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jeopardise</strong></a> local water supplies.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">破坏性采矿可能危及野生生物并威胁当地供水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Building on unstable slopes may endanger lives and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jeopardise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jeopardise</strong></a> recovery after a destructive storm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在不稳定坡地建房可能危及生命，并损害破坏性风暴后的恢复工作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">jeopardise safety</span> · 危及安全</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=threaten&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">threaten</a></span> · 威胁</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=damage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">damage</a></span> · 损害</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destructive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Destructive</strong></a> mining practices can endanger wildlife and jeopardise local water supplies.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">破坏性采矿可能危及野生生物并威胁当地供水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Building on unstable slopes may endanger lives and jeopardise recovery after a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destructive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>destructive</strong></a> storm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在不稳定坡地建房可能危及生命，并损害破坏性风暴后的恢复工作。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-022">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-022" class="vocabulary-word-title">El Nino<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-022" aria-label="定位 El Nino">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-022" data-mark-label="El Nino" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="El Nino：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="El Nino：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=El%20Nino&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 El Nino" title="在有道查询 El Nino">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-022</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 厄尔尼诺现象</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=El%20Nino&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>El Nino</strong></a> is a natural phenomenon that interacts with a climate altered by greenhouse gases.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">厄尔尼诺是一种自然现象，会与被温室气体改变的气候相互作用。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lecture distinguished the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=El%20Nino&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>El Nino</strong></a> phenomenon from the greenhouse effect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">讲座区分了厄尔尼诺现象与温室效应。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists examine how greenhouse warming may affect the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=El%20Nino&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>El Nino</strong></a> phenomenon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家研究温室变暖可能如何影响厄尔尼诺现象。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an El Nino event</span> · 厄尔尼诺事件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=climate%20system&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">climate system</a></span> · 气候系统</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=warming&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">warming</a></span> · 变暖</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 温室，暖房</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">El Nino is a natural phenomenon that interacts with a climate altered by <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=greenhouse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>greenhouse</strong></a> gases.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">厄尔尼诺是一种自然现象，会与被温室气体改变的气候相互作用。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lecture distinguished the El Nino phenomenon from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=greenhouse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>greenhouse</strong></a> effect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">讲座区分了厄尔尼诺现象与温室效应。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists examine how <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=greenhouse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>greenhouse</strong></a> warming may affect the El Nino phenomenon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家研究温室变暖可能如何影响厄尔尼诺现象。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">El Nino is a natural <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=phenomenon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>phenomenon</strong></a> that interacts with a climate altered by greenhouse gases.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">厄尔尼诺是一种自然现象，会与被温室气体改变的气候相互作用。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lecture distinguished the El Nino <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=phenomenon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>phenomenon</strong></a> from the greenhouse effect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">讲座区分了厄尔尼诺现象与温室效应。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-039">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-039" class="vocabulary-word-title">volcano<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-039" aria-label="定位 volcano">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-039" data-mark-label="volcano" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="volcano：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="volcano：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=volcano&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 volcano" title="在有道查询 volcano">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-039</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 火山</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Thermodynamic changes in magma can help explain when a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=volcano&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>volcano</strong></a> may erupt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">岩浆的热力学变化有助于解释火山何时可能喷发。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists model thermodynamic processes as magma rises inside a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=volcano&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>volcano</strong></a> that could erupt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家模拟可能喷发的火山内岩浆上升时的热力学过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=volcano&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>volcano</strong></a> may erupt when magma pressure rises, though thermodynamic models cannot predict every event.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">岩浆压力升高时火山可能喷发，但热力学模型无法预测每次喷发。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an active volcano</span> · 活火山</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lava&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lava</a></span> · 熔岩</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=geothermal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">geothermal</a></span> · 地热的</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-040">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-040" class="vocabulary-word-title">erupt<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-040" aria-label="定位 erupt">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-040" data-mark-label="erupt" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="erupt：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="erupt：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=erupt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 erupt" title="在有道查询 erupt">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-040</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 爆发，喷发；(斑疹等)突然出现</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Thermodynamic changes in magma can help explain when a volcano may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=erupt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>erupt</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">岩浆的热力学变化有助于解释火山何时可能喷发。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists model thermodynamic processes as magma rises inside a volcano that could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=erupt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>erupt</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家模拟可能喷发的火山内岩浆上升时的热力学过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A volcano may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=erupt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>erupt</strong></a> when magma pressure rises, though thermodynamic models cannot predict every event.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">岩浆压力升高时火山可能喷发，但热力学模型无法预测每次喷发。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">erupt violently</span> · 猛烈喷发</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lava&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lava</a></span> · 熔岩</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=geothermal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">geothermal</a></span> · 地热的</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-041">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-041" class="vocabulary-word-title">magma<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-041" aria-label="定位 magma">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-041" data-mark-label="magma" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="magma：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="magma：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 magma" title="在有道查询 magma">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-041</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 岩浆</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Thermodynamic changes in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>magma</strong></a> can help explain when a volcano may erupt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">岩浆的热力学变化有助于解释火山何时可能喷发。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists model thermodynamic processes as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>magma</strong></a> rises inside a volcano that could erupt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家模拟可能喷发的火山内岩浆上升时的热力学过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A volcano may erupt when <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>magma</strong></a> pressure rises, though thermodynamic models cannot predict every event.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">岩浆压力升高时火山可能喷发，但热力学模型无法预测每次喷发。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">molten magma</span> · 熔融岩浆</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lava&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lava</a></span> · 熔岩</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=geothermal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">geothermal</a></span> · 地热的</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-042">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-042" class="vocabulary-word-title">thermodynamic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-042" aria-label="定位 thermodynamic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-042" data-mark-label="thermodynamic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="thermodynamic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="thermodynamic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thermodynamic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 thermodynamic" title="在有道查询 thermodynamic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-042</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 热力的；热力学的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thermodynamic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Thermodynamic</strong></a> changes in magma can help explain when a volcano may erupt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">岩浆的热力学变化有助于解释火山何时可能喷发。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists model <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thermodynamic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>thermodynamic</strong></a> processes as magma rises inside a volcano that could erupt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家模拟可能喷发的火山内岩浆上升时的热力学过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A volcano may erupt when magma pressure rises, though <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thermodynamic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>thermodynamic</strong></a> models cannot predict every event.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">岩浆压力升高时火山可能喷发，但热力学模型无法预测每次喷发。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">thermodynamic processes</span> · 热力学过程</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lava&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lava</a></span> · 熔岩</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=geothermal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">geothermal</a></span> · 地热的</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-046">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-046" class="vocabulary-word-title">tsunami<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-046" aria-label="定位 tsunami">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-046" data-mark-label="tsunami" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tsunami：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tsunami：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tsunami&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tsunami" title="在有道查询 tsunami">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-046</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 海啸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After years of drought, a torrent caused flooding, while the coast faced a separate <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tsunami&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tsunami</strong></a> warning.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">多年干旱后，一股急流引发洪灾，海岸则另行面临海啸警报。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan addresses drought and flooding, including damage from a mountain torrent or coastal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tsunami&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tsunami</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划应对干旱和洪灾，包括山区急流或沿海海啸造成的破坏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tsunami&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tsunami</strong></a> differs from flooding caused by a torrent, and neither necessarily ends a regional drought.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">海啸不同于急流造成的洪灾，两者也不一定终结地区干旱。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a devastating tsunami</span> · 毁灭性海啸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inundation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inundation</a></span> · 淹没</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=water%20scarcity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">water scarcity</a></span> · 缺水</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-047">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-047" class="vocabulary-word-title">drought<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-047" aria-label="定位 drought">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-047" data-mark-label="drought" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="drought：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="drought：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drought&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 drought" title="在有道查询 drought">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-047</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 干旱，旱灾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After years of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drought&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drought</strong></a>, a torrent caused flooding, while the coast faced a separate tsunami warning.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">多年干旱后，一股急流引发洪灾，海岸则另行面临海啸警报。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan addresses <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drought&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drought</strong></a> and flooding, including damage from a mountain torrent or coastal tsunami.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划应对干旱和洪灾，包括山区急流或沿海海啸造成的破坏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tsunami differs from flooding caused by a torrent, and neither necessarily ends a regional <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drought&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drought</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">海啸不同于急流造成的洪灾，两者也不一定终结地区干旱。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a prolonged drought</span> · 长期干旱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inundation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inundation</a></span> · 淹没</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=water%20scarcity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">water scarcity</a></span> · 缺水</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-048">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-048" class="vocabulary-word-title">flooding<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-048" aria-label="定位 flooding">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-048" data-mark-label="flooding" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="flooding：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="flooding：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flooding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 flooding" title="在有道查询 flooding">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-048</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 洪水泛滥</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After years of drought, a torrent caused <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flooding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flooding</strong></a>, while the coast faced a separate tsunami warning.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">多年干旱后，一股急流引发洪灾，海岸则另行面临海啸警报。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan addresses drought and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flooding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flooding</strong></a>, including damage from a mountain torrent or coastal tsunami.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划应对干旱和洪灾，包括山区急流或沿海海啸造成的破坏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tsunami differs from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flooding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flooding</strong></a> caused by a torrent, and neither necessarily ends a regional drought.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">海啸不同于急流造成的洪灾，两者也不一定终结地区干旱。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">severe flooding</span> · 严重洪灾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inundation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inundation</a></span> · 淹没</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=water%20scarcity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">water scarcity</a></span> · 缺水</li></ul>
</div>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan addresses drought and flooding, including damage from a mountain <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=torrent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>torrent</strong></a> or coastal tsunami.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划应对干旱和洪灾，包括山区急流或沿海海啸造成的破坏。</p></li>

</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a raging torrent</span> · 汹涌急流</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-050">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-050" class="vocabulary-word-title">earthquake<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-050" aria-label="定位 earthquake">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-050" data-mark-label="earthquake" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="earthquake：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="earthquake：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=earthquake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 earthquake" title="在有道查询 earthquake">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-050</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 地震</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Seismic shaking from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=earthquake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>earthquake</strong></a> triggered an avalanche.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地震产生的震动引发了雪崩。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The seismic station recorded the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=earthquake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>earthquake</strong></a> moments before an avalanche blocked the valley.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地震台记录地震后不久，一场雪崩堵塞山谷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=earthquake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>earthquake</strong></a> can cause an avalanche even where seismic risk is usually considered low.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使在通常被认为地震风险低的地方，地震也可能引起雪崩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a major earthquake</span> · 大地震</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tectonic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tectonic</a></span> · 地壳构造的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landslide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">landslide</a></span> · 滑坡</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-051">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-051" class="vocabulary-word-title">seismic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-051" aria-label="定位 seismic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-051" data-mark-label="seismic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="seismic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="seismic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seismic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 seismic" title="在有道查询 seismic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-051</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 地震的，地震引起的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seismic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Seismic</strong></a> shaking from the earthquake triggered an avalanche.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地震产生的震动引发了雪崩。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seismic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seismic</strong></a> station recorded the earthquake moments before an avalanche blocked the valley.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地震台记录地震后不久，一场雪崩堵塞山谷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An earthquake can cause an avalanche even where <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seismic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seismic</strong></a> risk is usually considered low.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使在通常被认为地震风险低的地方，地震也可能引起雪崩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">seismic activity</span> · 地震活动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tectonic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tectonic</a></span> · 地壳构造的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landslide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">landslide</a></span> · 滑坡</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-052">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-052" class="vocabulary-word-title">avalanche<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-052" aria-label="定位 avalanche">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-052" data-mark-label="avalanche" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="avalanche：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="avalanche：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=avalanche&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 avalanche" title="在有道查询 avalanche">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-052</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 雪崩</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Seismic shaking from the earthquake triggered an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=avalanche&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>avalanche</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地震产生的震动引发了雪崩。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The seismic station recorded the earthquake moments before an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=avalanche&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>avalanche</strong></a> blocked the valley.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地震台记录地震后不久，一场雪崩堵塞山谷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An earthquake can cause an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=avalanche&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>avalanche</strong></a> even where seismic risk is usually considered low.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使在通常被认为地震风险低的地方，地震也可能引起雪崩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a snow avalanche</span> · 雪崩</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tectonic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tectonic</a></span> · 地壳构造的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landslide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">landslide</a></span> · 滑坡</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 01.13｜空气与环境污染 {#group-01-13}

**串联方式：** 辨认烟雾和废气，联系污染物及有毒物质；mist 本身不等于污染。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-043">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-043" class="vocabulary-word-title">smog<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-043" aria-label="定位 smog">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-043" data-mark-label="smog" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="smog：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="smog：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=smog&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 smog" title="在有道查询 smog">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-043</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 烟雾，雾霾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Morning mist obscured the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=smog&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>smog</strong></a> outside as laboratory staff worked beneath a fume hood.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">晨雾遮住了室外烟雾，实验室人员则在通风橱下工作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The monitor distinguished <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=smog&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>smog</strong></a> from mist, while a fume extractor removed gases indoors.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监测器区分烟雾与薄雾，室内则用排气装置清除气体。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike harmless mist, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=smog&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>smog</strong></a> can contain hazardous gases that require a fume extraction system in workplaces.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与无害薄雾不同，烟雾可能含有有害气体，工作场所需用排气系统处理。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">urban smog</span> · 城市烟雾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exhaust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">exhaust</a></span> · 废气</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=visibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">visibility</a></span> · 能见度</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-044">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-044" class="vocabulary-word-title">fume<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-044" aria-label="定位 fume">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-044" data-mark-label="fume" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fume：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fume：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fume" title="在有道查询 fume">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-044</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [~s](难闻且常为有害的)烟，气体；v. 冒烟；发怒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Morning mist obscured the smog outside as laboratory staff worked beneath a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fume</strong></a> hood.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">晨雾遮住了室外烟雾，实验室人员则在通风橱下工作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The monitor distinguished smog from mist, while a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fume</strong></a> extractor removed gases indoors.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监测器区分烟雾与薄雾，室内则用排气装置清除气体。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike harmless mist, smog can contain hazardous gases that require a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fume</strong></a> extraction system in workplaces.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与无害薄雾不同，烟雾可能含有有害气体，工作场所需用排气系统处理。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a fume hood</span> · 通风橱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exhaust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">exhaust</a></span> · 废气</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=visibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">visibility</a></span> · 能见度</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-045">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-045" class="vocabulary-word-title">mist<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-045" aria-label="定位 mist">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-045" data-mark-label="mist" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mist：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mist：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mist" title="在有道查询 mist">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-045</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 薄雾；水汽；使视线模糊的东西</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Morning <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mist</strong></a> obscured the smog outside as laboratory staff worked beneath a fume hood.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">晨雾遮住了室外烟雾，实验室人员则在通风橱下工作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The monitor distinguished smog from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mist</strong></a>, while a fume extractor removed gases indoors.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监测器区分烟雾与薄雾，室内则用排气装置清除气体。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike harmless <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mist</strong></a>, smog can contain hazardous gases that require a fume extraction system in workplaces.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与无害薄雾不同，烟雾可能含有有害气体，工作场所需用排气系统处理。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">morning mist</span> · 晨雾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exhaust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">exhaust</a></span> · 废气</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=visibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">visibility</a></span> · 能见度</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-172">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-172" class="vocabulary-word-title">toxic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-172" aria-label="定位 toxic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-172" data-mark-label="toxic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="toxic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="toxic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=toxic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 toxic" title="在有道查询 toxic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-172</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 有毒的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=toxic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>toxic</strong></a> pollutant can contaminate groundwater and cause long-term pollution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有毒污染物可能污染地下水并造成长期污染。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Factories must control <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=toxic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>toxic</strong></a> pollution so that no pollutant can escape and contaminate rivers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工厂必须控制有毒污染，防止任何污染物泄漏并污染河流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers traced the pollution to a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=toxic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>toxic</strong></a> pollutant that could contaminate nearby wells.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员追溯出污染源是一种可能污染附近水井的有毒污染物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">toxic waste</span> · 有毒废物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=toxicity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">toxicity</a></span> · 毒性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contamination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contamination</a></span> · 污染</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-173">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-173" class="vocabulary-word-title">pollution<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-173" aria-label="定位 pollution">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-173" data-mark-label="pollution" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pollution：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pollution：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pollution" title="在有道查询 pollution">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-173</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 污染</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A toxic pollutant can contaminate groundwater and cause long-term <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pollution</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有毒污染物可能污染地下水并造成长期污染。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Factories must control toxic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pollution</strong></a> so that no pollutant can escape and contaminate rivers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工厂必须控制有毒污染，防止任何污染物泄漏并污染河流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers traced the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pollution</strong></a> to a toxic pollutant that could contaminate nearby wells.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员追溯出污染源是一种可能污染附近水井的有毒污染物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">water pollution</span> · 水污染</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=toxicity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">toxicity</a></span> · 毒性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contamination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contamination</a></span> · 污染</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-174">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-174" class="vocabulary-word-title">pollutant<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-174" aria-label="定位 pollutant">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-174" data-mark-label="pollutant" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pollutant：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pollutant：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollutant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pollutant" title="在有道查询 pollutant">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-174</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 污染物质</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A toxic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollutant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pollutant</strong></a> can contaminate groundwater and cause long-term pollution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有毒污染物可能污染地下水并造成长期污染。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Factories must control toxic pollution so that no <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollutant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pollutant</strong></a> can escape and contaminate rivers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工厂必须控制有毒污染，防止任何污染物泄漏并污染河流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers traced the pollution to a toxic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollutant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pollutant</strong></a> that could contaminate nearby wells.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员追溯出污染源是一种可能污染附近水井的有毒污染物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a persistent pollutant</span> · 持久性污染物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=toxicity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">toxicity</a></span> · 毒性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contamination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contamination</a></span> · 污染</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A toxic pollutant can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contaminate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contaminate</strong></a> groundwater and cause long-term pollution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有毒污染物可能污染地下水并造成长期污染。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Factories must control toxic pollution so that no pollutant can escape and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contaminate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contaminate</strong></a> rivers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工厂必须控制有毒污染，防止任何污染物泄漏并污染河流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers traced the pollution to a toxic pollutant that could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contaminate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contaminate</strong></a> nearby wells.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员追溯出污染源是一种可能污染附近水井的有毒污染物。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 01.14｜土地退化与修复 {#group-01-14}

**串联方式：** 由荒野、沙漠和贫瘠土地谈到砍伐、侵蚀与恶化，再讨论肥力恢复和改善。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-072">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-072" class="vocabulary-word-title">deteriorate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-072" aria-label="定位 deteriorate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-072" data-mark-label="deteriorate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="deteriorate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="deteriorate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deteriorate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 deteriorate" title="在有道查询 deteriorate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-072</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 恶化，变坏</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As conditions <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deteriorate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deteriorate</strong></a>, storms erode banks and degrade soil, so we must upgrade drainage rather than aggravate runoff.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">情况恶化时，风暴侵蚀河岸并使土壤退化，因此我们必须升级排水，而非加剧径流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Poor roads aggravate flooding, erode slopes and degrade habitats; an upgrade can help before conditions <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deteriorate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deteriorate</strong></a> further.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">劣质道路加剧洪水、侵蚀斜坡并破坏栖息地，升级可在情况进一步恶化前有所帮助。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To stop land quality from continuing to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deteriorate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deteriorate</strong></a>, upgrade channels that erode banks, aggravate runoff and degrade wetlands.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要阻止土地质量持续恶化，需改造那些侵蚀河岸、加剧径流并损害湿地的水道。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">deteriorate rapidly</span> · 迅速恶化</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=degradation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">degradation</a></span> · 退化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restoration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">restoration</a></span> · 修复</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-073">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-073" class="vocabulary-word-title">aggravate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-073" aria-label="定位 aggravate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-073" data-mark-label="aggravate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="aggravate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="aggravate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggravate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 aggravate" title="在有道查询 aggravate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-073</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
