<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As conditions deteriorate, storms erode banks and degrade soil, so we must upgrade drainage rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggravate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aggravate</strong></a> runoff.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">情况恶化时，风暴侵蚀河岸并使土壤退化，因此我们必须升级排水，而非加剧径流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Poor roads <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggravate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aggravate</strong></a> flooding, erode slopes and degrade habitats; an upgrade can help before conditions deteriorate further.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">劣质道路加剧洪水、侵蚀斜坡并破坏栖息地，升级可在情况进一步恶化前有所帮助。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To stop land quality from continuing to deteriorate, upgrade channels that erode banks, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggravate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aggravate</strong></a> runoff and degrade wetlands.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要阻止土地质量持续恶化，需改造那些侵蚀河岸、加剧径流并损害湿地的水道。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=degradation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">degradation</a></span> · 退化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restoration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">restoration</a></span> · 修复</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To stop land quality from continuing to deteriorate, upgrade channels that erode banks, aggravate runoff and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=degrade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>degrade</strong></a> wetlands.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要阻止土地质量持续恶化，需改造那些侵蚀河岸、加剧径流并损害湿地的水道。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-190">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-190" class="vocabulary-word-title">wilderness<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-190" aria-label="定位 wilderness">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-190" data-mark-label="wilderness" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wilderness：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wilderness：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wilderness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wilderness" title="在有道查询 wilderness">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-190</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 荒野</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If developers deforest this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wilderness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wilderness</strong></a>, dry land may eventually become desert.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若开发者砍伐这片荒野的林木，干旱土地最终可能变成沙漠。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan would deforest land bordering the desert and reduce the remaining <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wilderness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wilderness</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这项计划会砍伐沙漠边缘林地，并缩小剩余荒野。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Not every desert results from damage, but attempts to deforest dry <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wilderness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wilderness</strong></a> can worsen land degradation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">并非所有沙漠都由破坏造成，但砍伐干旱荒野中的林木可能加剧土地退化。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">unspoilt wilderness</span> · 未受破坏的荒野</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deforestation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">deforestation</a></span> · 毁林</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desertification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">desertification</a></span> · 荒漠化</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-191">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-191" class="vocabulary-word-title">desert<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-191" aria-label="定位 desert">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-191" data-mark-label="desert" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="desert：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="desert：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 desert" title="在有道查询 desert">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-191</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 沙漠；v. 离弃，遗弃；擅离</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If developers deforest this wilderness, dry land may eventually become <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>desert</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若开发者砍伐这片荒野的林木，干旱土地最终可能变成沙漠。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan would deforest land bordering the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>desert</strong></a> and reduce the remaining wilderness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这项计划会砍伐沙漠边缘林地，并缩小剩余荒野。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Not every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>desert</strong></a> results from damage, but attempts to deforest dry wilderness can worsen land degradation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">并非所有沙漠都由破坏造成，但砍伐干旱荒野中的林木可能加剧土地退化。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a hot desert</span> · 炎热沙漠</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deforestation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">deforestation</a></span> · 毁林</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desertification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">desertification</a></span> · 荒漠化</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-192">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-192" class="vocabulary-word-title">deforest<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-192" aria-label="定位 deforest">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-192" data-mark-label="deforest" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="deforest：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="deforest：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deforest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 deforest" title="在有道查询 deforest">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-192</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 毁掉……森林</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If developers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deforest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deforest</strong></a> this wilderness, dry land may eventually become desert.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若开发者砍伐这片荒野的林木，干旱土地最终可能变成沙漠。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan would <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deforest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deforest</strong></a> land bordering the desert and reduce the remaining wilderness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这项计划会砍伐沙漠边缘林地，并缩小剩余荒野。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Not every desert results from damage, but attempts to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deforest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deforest</strong></a> dry wilderness can worsen land degradation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">并非所有沙漠都由破坏造成，但砍伐干旱荒野中的林木可能加剧土地退化。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">deforest a hillside</span> · 砍伐山坡林木</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deforestation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">deforestation</a></span> · 毁林</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desertification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">desertification</a></span> · 荒漠化</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-193">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-193" class="vocabulary-word-title">barren<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-193" aria-label="定位 barren">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-193" data-mark-label="barren" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="barren：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="barren：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barren&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 barren" title="在有道查询 barren">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-193</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 贫瘠的，荒芜的；不结果实的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Farmers fertilise the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barren&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barren</strong></a> plot to make its soil more fertile.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民给贫瘠地块施肥，使土壤更肥沃。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A fertile field can become <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barren&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barren</strong></a> if growers fertilise carelessly and damage its soil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若种植者不当施肥并损坏土壤，肥沃田地可能变得贫瘠。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Compost helps fertilise <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barren&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barren</strong></a> ground, but restoring fertile soil also requires water and time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">堆肥帮助贫瘠土地增加养分，但恢复肥沃土壤还需水和时间。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">barren land</span> · 贫瘠土地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fertility</a></span> · 肥力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compost</a></span> · 堆肥</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-194">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-194" class="vocabulary-word-title">fertile<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-194" aria-label="定位 fertile">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-194" data-mark-label="fertile" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fertile：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fertile：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fertile" title="在有道查询 fertile">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-194</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 肥沃的，富饶的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Farmers fertilise the barren plot to make its soil more <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fertile</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民给贫瘠地块施肥，使土壤更肥沃。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fertile</strong></a> field can become barren if growers fertilise carelessly and damage its soil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若种植者不当施肥并损坏土壤，肥沃田地可能变得贫瘠。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Compost helps fertilise barren ground, but restoring <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fertile</strong></a> soil also requires water and time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">堆肥帮助贫瘠土地增加养分，但恢复肥沃土壤还需水和时间。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">fertile soil</span> · 肥沃土壤</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fertility</a></span> · 肥力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compost</a></span> · 堆肥</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-195">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-195" class="vocabulary-word-title">fertilise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-195" aria-label="定位 fertilise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-195" data-mark-label="fertilise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fertilise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fertilise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertilise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fertilise" title="在有道查询 fertilise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-195</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 施肥于(= fertilize)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Farmers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertilise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fertilise</strong></a> the barren plot to make its soil more fertile.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民给贫瘠地块施肥，使土壤更肥沃。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A fertile field can become barren if growers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertilise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fertilise</strong></a> carelessly and damage its soil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若种植者不当施肥并损坏土壤，肥沃田地可能变得贫瘠。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Compost helps <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertilise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fertilise</strong></a> barren ground, but restoring fertile soil also requires water and time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">堆肥帮助贫瘠土地增加养分，但恢复肥沃土壤还需水和时间。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">fertilise a field</span> · 给田地施肥</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fertility</a></span> · 肥力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compost</a></span> · 堆肥</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 01.15｜描述方向、距离与边界 {#group-01-15}

**串联方式：** 先说方位，再说城乡与远近，最后用边缘、板块、碎片和裂缝定位局部；plate 也有“盘子”义。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-148">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-148" class="vocabulary-word-title">southern<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-148" aria-label="定位 southern">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-148" data-mark-label="southern" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="southern：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="southern：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=southern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 southern" title="在有道查询 southern">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-148</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 南部的；南方的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=southern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>southern</strong></a> route runs from the southeast coast to the southwest hills, bypassing northeast and northwest districts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">南线从东南海岸通往西南丘陵，绕过东北和西北地区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=southern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Southern</strong></a> rainfall spread from the southwest to the southeast, while the northeast and northwest stayed dry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">南部降雨从西南扩展到东南，东北和西北仍干燥。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The map divides the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=southern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>southern</strong></a> region into southeast and southwest zones below its northeast and northwest neighbours.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地图将南部地区划为东南和西南两区，位于东北和西北相邻地区下方。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">southern regions</span> · 南部地区</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compass&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compass</a></span> · 指南针</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bearing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bearing</a></span> · 方向角</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-149">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-149" class="vocabulary-word-title">southeast<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-149" aria-label="定位 southeast">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-149" data-mark-label="southeast" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="southeast：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="southeast：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=southeast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 southeast" title="在有道查询 southeast">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-149</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 东南方；东南部；adj. 东南方的；东南部的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The southern route runs from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=southeast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>southeast</strong></a> coast to the southwest hills, bypassing northeast and northwest districts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">南线从东南海岸通往西南丘陵，绕过东北和西北地区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Southern rainfall spread from the southwest to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=southeast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>southeast</strong></a>, while the northeast and northwest stayed dry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">南部降雨从西南扩展到东南，东北和西北仍干燥。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The map divides the southern region into <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=southeast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>southeast</strong></a> and southwest zones below its northeast and northwest neighbours.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地图将南部地区划为东南和西南两区，位于东北和西北相邻地区下方。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the southeast coast</span> · 东南海岸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compass&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compass</a></span> · 指南针</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bearing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bearing</a></span> · 方向角</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Southern rainfall spread from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=southwest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>southwest</strong></a> to the southeast, while the northeast and northwest stayed dry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">南部降雨从西南扩展到东南，东北和西北仍干燥。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-153">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-153" class="vocabulary-word-title">eastern<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-153" aria-label="定位 eastern">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-153" data-mark-label="eastern" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="eastern：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="eastern：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eastern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 eastern" title="在有道查询 eastern">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-153</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 东部的；东方的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The gallery on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eastern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eastern</strong></a> waterfront was historically advertised as a museum of oriental art.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">东部滨水区的画廊曾以东方艺术博物馆为名进行宣传。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An old catalogue calls objects from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eastern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eastern</strong></a> Mediterranean oriental, reflecting the terminology of its period.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旧目录把东地中海物件称为“东方的”，反映了当时的用语。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eastern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eastern</strong></a> wing now uses specific regional labels instead of the broad historical category of oriental art.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">东翼如今使用具体地区标签，取代过去笼统的“东方艺术”分类。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the eastern coast</span> · 东海岸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=East%20Asian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">East Asian</a></span> · 东亚的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=easternmost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">easternmost</a></span> · 最东端的</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-154">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-154" class="vocabulary-word-title">oriental<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-154" aria-label="定位 oriental">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-154" data-mark-label="oriental" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="oriental：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="oriental：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oriental&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 oriental" title="在有道查询 oriental">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-154</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 东方的(尤指中国和日本的)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-usage"><span class="vocabulary-label">用法提示</span> 本组将 oriental 用于旧称或历史艺术风格；现代描述具体地区时，可使用更明确的地域名称。</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The gallery on the eastern waterfront was historically advertised as a museum of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oriental&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oriental</strong></a> art.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">东部滨水区的画廊曾以东方艺术博物馆为名进行宣传。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An old catalogue calls objects from the eastern Mediterranean <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oriental&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oriental</strong></a>, reflecting the terminology of its period.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旧目录把东地中海物件称为“东方的”，反映了当时的用语。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The eastern wing now uses specific regional labels instead of the broad historical category of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oriental&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oriental</strong></a> art.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">东翼如今使用具体地区标签，取代过去笼统的“东方艺术”分类。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">oriental art</span> · 东方艺术</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=East%20Asian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">East Asian</a></span> · 东亚的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=easternmost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">easternmost</a></span> · 最东端的</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-165">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-165" class="vocabulary-word-title">rural<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-165" aria-label="定位 rural">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-165" data-mark-label="rural" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rural：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rural：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rural&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rural" title="在有道查询 rural">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-165</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 农村的，乡村的；田园的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Beyond the suburb and city outskirts, a remote road connects <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rural&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rural</strong></a> communities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">越过郊区和城市外围，一条偏僻道路连接农村社区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bus links a remote <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rural&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rural</strong></a> village to the outskirts and then a residential suburb.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公交连接一座偏远农村村庄、城市外围及住宅郊区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Housing spread from the suburb into the outskirts, while remote <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rural&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rural</strong></a> areas continued to lose residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">住宅从郊区扩展到城市外围，而偏远农村地区继续流失居民。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">rural communities</span> · 农村社区</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=urban&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">urban</a></span> · 城市的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">commute</a></span> · 通勤</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-166">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-166" class="vocabulary-word-title">suburb<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-166" aria-label="定位 suburb">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-166" data-mark-label="suburb" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="suburb：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="suburb：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suburb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 suburb" title="在有道查询 suburb">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-166</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 郊区，郊外，近郊</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Beyond the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suburb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suburb</strong></a> and city outskirts, a remote road connects rural communities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">越过郊区和城市外围，一条偏僻道路连接农村社区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bus links a remote rural village to the outskirts and then a residential <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suburb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suburb</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公交连接一座偏远农村村庄、城市外围及住宅郊区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Housing spread from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suburb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suburb</strong></a> into the outskirts, while remote rural areas continued to lose residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">住宅从郊区扩展到城市外围，而偏远农村地区继续流失居民。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a residential suburb</span> · 住宅郊区</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=urban&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">urban</a></span> · 城市的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">commute</a></span> · 通勤</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-169">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 荒凉的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desolate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>desolate</strong></a> village occupies land adjacent to the road beneath a distant mountain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">荒凉村庄位于远山下、毗邻道路的土地上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Distant lights were visible from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desolate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>desolate</strong></a> plain adjacent to the abandoned airfield.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">从废弃机场旁的荒凉平原上可以看见远方灯光。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey compared a distant <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desolate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>desolate</strong></a> island with the inhabited mainland and adjacent waters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查将一座遥远荒岛与有人居住的大陆及相邻水域比较。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a desolate landscape</span> · 荒凉景观</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=isolated&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">isolated</a></span> · 孤立的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nearby&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">nearby</a></span> · 附近的</li></ul>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=border&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>border</strong></a> follows the river margin along the forest fringe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">边界沿森林边缘的河岸延伸。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Settlement on the forest fringe approached the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=border&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>border</strong></a> at the lake's eastern margin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">森林边缘的聚居地逐渐接近湖泊东缘的边界。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We surveyed the woodland fringe, the river margin and the international <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=border&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>border</strong></a> separately.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们分别调查林地边缘、河流边缘及国界。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The border follows the river <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=margin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>margin</strong></a> along the forest fringe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">边界沿森林边缘的河岸延伸。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Settlement on the forest fringe approached the border at the lake's eastern <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=margin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>margin</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">森林边缘的聚居地逐渐接近湖泊东缘的边界。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We surveyed the woodland fringe, the river <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=margin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>margin</strong></a> and the international border separately.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们分别调查林地边缘、河流边缘及国界。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the river margin</span> · 河缘</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boundary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">boundary</a></span> · 边界</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=edge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">edge</a></span> · 边缘</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-180">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-180" class="vocabulary-word-title">plate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-180" aria-label="定位 plate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-180" data-mark-label="plate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="plate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="plate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 plate" title="在有道查询 plate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-180</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(地质学中的大陆)板块；盘</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Movement of a tectonic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plate</strong></a> opened a crack and a gap that filled with debris.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">构造板块运动造成裂缝和缺口，随后被碎石填满。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Debris fell into the gap as a crack widened near the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plate</strong></a> boundary.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">板块边界附近的裂缝扩大时，碎石落入缺口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The model shows a moving <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plate</strong></a>, a widening crack, scattered debris and a gap between rock faces.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模型展示移动的板块、扩大的裂缝、散落的碎石及岩壁间的缺口。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a tectonic plate</span> · 构造板块</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fracture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fracture</a></span> · 断裂</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fault%20line&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fault line</a></span> · 断层线</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 01.16｜评价变化与景观 {#group-01-16}

**串联方式：** 分别评价变化是否可逆、是否异常，以及景观是否壮观；评价词不构成因果关系。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-155">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-155" class="vocabulary-word-title">inevitable<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-155" aria-label="定位 inevitable">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-155" data-mark-label="inevitable" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="inevitable：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="inevitable：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inevitable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 inevitable" title="在有道查询 inevitable">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-155</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 不可避免的，必然的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Storms occur irregularly, but continued erosion makes some irreversible loss <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inevitable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>inevitable</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">风暴不定期发生，但持续侵蚀使某些不可逆损失不可避免。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Although rainfall arrives irregularly, irreversible desertification is not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inevitable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>inevitable</strong></a> if land is protected.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尽管降雨不规律，若土地得到保护，不可逆荒漠化并非不可避免。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The glacier retreats irregularly, yet an irreversible change now appears <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inevitable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>inevitable</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冰川不规则地退缩，但如今不可逆变化似乎不可避免。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">inevitable change</span> · 不可避免的变化</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reversible&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reversible</a></span> · 可逆的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=variability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">variability</a></span> · 变化性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-156">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-156" class="vocabulary-word-title">irreversible<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-156" aria-label="定位 irreversible">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-156" data-mark-label="irreversible" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="irreversible：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="irreversible：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irreversible&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 irreversible" title="在有道查询 irreversible">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-156</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 不可逆转的；不可挽回的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Storms occur irregularly, but continued erosion makes some <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irreversible&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>irreversible</strong></a> loss inevitable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">风暴不定期发生，但持续侵蚀使某些不可逆损失不可避免。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Although rainfall arrives irregularly, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irreversible&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>irreversible</strong></a> desertification is not inevitable if land is protected.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尽管降雨不规律，若土地得到保护，不可逆荒漠化并非不可避免。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The glacier retreats irregularly, yet an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irreversible&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>irreversible</strong></a> change now appears inevitable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冰川不规则地退缩，但如今不可逆变化似乎不可避免。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">irreversible damage</span> · 不可逆的损害</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reversible&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reversible</a></span> · 可逆的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=variability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">variability</a></span> · 变化性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-157">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-157" class="vocabulary-word-title">irregularly<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-157" aria-label="定位 irregularly">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-157" data-mark-label="irregularly" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="irregularly：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="irregularly：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irregularly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 irregularly" title="在有道查询 irregularly">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-157</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adv. 不规则地；不合常规地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Storms occur <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irregularly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>irregularly</strong></a>, but continued erosion makes some irreversible loss inevitable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">风暴不定期发生，但持续侵蚀使某些不可逆损失不可避免。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Although rainfall arrives <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irregularly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>irregularly</strong></a>, irreversible desertification is not inevitable if land is protected.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尽管降雨不规律，若土地得到保护，不可逆荒漠化并非不可避免。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The glacier retreats <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irregularly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>irregularly</strong></a>, yet an irreversible change now appears inevitable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冰川不规则地退缩，但如今不可逆变化似乎不可避免。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">occur irregularly</span> · 不定期发生</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reversible&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reversible</a></span> · 可逆的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=variability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">variability</a></span> · 变化性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-158">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-158" class="vocabulary-word-title">inappropriate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-158" aria-label="定位 inappropriate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-158" data-mark-label="inappropriate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="inappropriate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="inappropriate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inappropriate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 inappropriate" title="在有道查询 inappropriate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-158</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 不合适的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inappropriate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Inappropriate</strong></a> irrigation worsened the damage caused by abnormal heat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不当灌溉加剧了异常高温造成的损害。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">It is <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inappropriate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>inappropriate</strong></a> to infer climate trends from one abnormal day.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">仅凭一个异常日来推断气候趋势是不恰当的。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report blamed <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inappropriate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>inappropriate</strong></a> land use and abnormal rainfall for the flooding.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告将洪灾归因于不当土地利用和异常降雨。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">inappropriate development</span> · 不当开发</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-159">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-159" class="vocabulary-word-title">abnormal<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-159" aria-label="定位 abnormal">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-159" data-mark-label="abnormal" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="abnormal：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="abnormal：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=abnormal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 abnormal" title="在有道查询 abnormal">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-159</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 不正常的，反常的；变态的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inappropriate irrigation worsened the damage caused by <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=abnormal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>abnormal</strong></a> heat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不当灌溉加剧了异常高温造成的损害。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">It is inappropriate to infer climate trends from one <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=abnormal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>abnormal</strong></a> day.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">仅凭一个异常日来推断气候趋势是不恰当的。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report blamed inappropriate land use and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=abnormal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>abnormal</strong></a> rainfall for the flooding.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告将洪灾归因于不当土地利用和异常降雨。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">abnormal temperatures</span> · 异常温度</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appropriate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">appropriate</a></span> · 适当的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=normal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">normal</a></span> · 正常的</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-184">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-184" class="vocabulary-word-title">splendid<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-184" aria-label="定位 splendid">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-184" data-mark-label="splendid" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="splendid：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="splendid：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=splendid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 splendid" title="在有道查询 splendid">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-184</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 极好的；壮观的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The magnificent view combined <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=splendid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>splendid</strong></a> waterfalls with the grand sweep of the valley.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">壮丽景观将绝美瀑布与山谷的宏阔曲线融为一体。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=splendid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>splendid</strong></a> trail led to a magnificent viewpoint above the grand canyon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一条绝美小径通往宏伟峡谷上方的壮丽观景点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors praised the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=splendid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>splendid</strong></a> gardens, magnificent cliffs and grand scale of the landscape.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游客赞叹绝美花园、壮丽悬崖及景观的宏大规模。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">splendid scenery</span> · 绝美景色</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breathtaking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">breathtaking</a></span> · 令人惊叹的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vista&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">vista</a></span> · 远景</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 宏大的；豪华的，宏伟的；极好的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The magnificent view combined splendid waterfalls with the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grand</strong></a> sweep of the valley.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">壮丽景观将绝美瀑布与山谷的宏阔曲线融为一体。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A splendid trail led to a magnificent viewpoint above the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grand</strong></a> canyon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一条绝美小径通往宏伟峡谷上方的壮丽观景点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors praised the splendid gardens, magnificent cliffs and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grand</strong></a> scale of the landscape.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游客赞叹绝美花园、壮丽悬崖及景观的宏大规模。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a grand canyon</span> · 宏伟峡谷</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breathtaking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">breathtaking</a></span> · 令人惊叹的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vista&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">vista</a></span> · 远景</li></ul>
</div>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 壮丽的，宏伟的；令人印象深刻的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnificent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>magnificent</strong></a> view combined splendid waterfalls with the grand sweep of the valley.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">壮丽景观将绝美瀑布与山谷的宏阔曲线融为一体。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A splendid trail led to a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnificent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>magnificent</strong></a> viewpoint above the grand canyon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一条绝美小径通往宏伟峡谷上方的壮丽观景点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors praised the splendid gardens, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnificent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>magnificent</strong></a> cliffs and grand scale of the landscape.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游客赞叹绝美花园、壮丽悬崖及景观的宏大规模。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a magnificent view</span> · 壮丽景观</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breathtaking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">breathtaking</a></span> · 令人惊叹的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vista&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">vista</a></span> · 远景</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-187">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-187" class="vocabulary-word-title">super<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-187" aria-label="定位 super">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-187" data-mark-label="super" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="super：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="super：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=super&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 super" title="在有道查询 super">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-187</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 超级的，极好的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We had a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=super&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>super</strong></a> view of an interesting rock arch and the dramatic cliffs beyond it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们极好地看到了有趣的岩拱及其后壮观的悬崖。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guide gave a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=super&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>super</strong></a> explanation of the interesting forces behind this dramatic landscape.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导游精彩地解释了塑造这片壮观景观的有趣力量。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">It was interesting to watch the dramatic sunset from such a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=super&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>super</strong></a> viewpoint.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">从如此棒的观景点欣赏壮丽日落很有意思。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 01.17｜太阳、月亮与明暗 {#group-01-17}

**串联方式：** 跟随日出、日落、黄昏和日食理解天体与时间，再区分光照、阴凉和影子。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-196">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-196" class="vocabulary-word-title">solar<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-196" aria-label="定位 solar">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-196" data-mark-label="solar" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="solar：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="solar：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 solar" title="在有道查询 solar">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-196</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 太阳的，日光的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The calendar lists dates for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solar</strong></a> eclipse and a lunar eclipse.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">日历列出了日食和月食的日期。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Astronomers used the calendar to plan observations of both a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solar</strong></a> and a lunar eclipse.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">天文学家使用日历安排日食和月食的观测。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A lunar eclipse differs from a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solar</strong></a> eclipse, though both can be predicted and marked on a calendar.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">月食与日食不同，但两者都能预测并标在日历上。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a solar eclipse</span> · 日食</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=orbit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">orbit</a></span> · 轨道</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alignment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">alignment</a></span> · 排列成线</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-197">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-197" class="vocabulary-word-title">lunar<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-197" aria-label="定位 lunar">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-197" data-mark-label="lunar" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="lunar：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="lunar：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lunar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 lunar" title="在有道查询 lunar">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-197</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The calendar lists dates for a solar eclipse and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lunar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lunar</strong></a> eclipse.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">日历列出了日食和月食的日期。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Astronomers used the calendar to plan observations of both a solar and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lunar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lunar</strong></a> eclipse.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">天文学家使用日历安排日食和月食的观测。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lunar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lunar</strong></a> eclipse differs from a solar eclipse, though both can be predicted and marked on a calendar.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">月食与日食不同，但两者都能预测并标在日历上。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a lunar eclipse</span> · 月食</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-199">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-199" class="vocabulary-word-title">sunrise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-199" aria-label="定位 sunrise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-199" data-mark-label="sunrise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sunrise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sunrise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunrise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sunrise" title="在有道查询 sunrise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-199</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 日出</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We left at <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunrise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sunrise</strong></a> and returned after sunset, when dusk softened the hills.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们日出时出发，日落后返回，此时暮色柔化了群山。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Bird counts began at <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunrise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sunrise</strong></a> and resumed around sunset before dusk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">鸟类计数从日出开始，并在日落前后的黄昏来临前恢复。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The camera recorded <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunrise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sunrise</strong></a>, sunset and the changing colours of dusk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">相机记录了日出、日落及黄昏变换的色彩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">at sunrise</span> · 日出时</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dawn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dawn</a></span> · 黎明</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twilight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">twilight</a></span> · 暮光</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-200">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-200" class="vocabulary-word-title">sunset<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-200" aria-label="定位 sunset">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-200" data-mark-label="sunset" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sunset：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sunset：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunset&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sunset" title="在有道查询 sunset">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-200</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 日落</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We left at sunrise and returned after <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunset&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sunset</strong></a>, when dusk softened the hills.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们日出时出发，日落后返回，此时暮色柔化了群山。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Bird counts began at sunrise and resumed around <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunset&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sunset</strong></a> before dusk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">鸟类计数从日出开始，并在日落前后的黄昏来临前恢复。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The camera recorded sunrise, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunset&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sunset</strong></a> and the changing colours of dusk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">相机记录了日出、日落及黄昏变换的色彩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">watch the sunset</span> · 看日落</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-201">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-201" class="vocabulary-word-title">eclipse<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-201" aria-label="定位 eclipse">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-201" data-mark-label="eclipse" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="eclipse：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="eclipse：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eclipse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 eclipse" title="在有道查询 eclipse">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-201</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 日食；月食</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The calendar lists dates for a solar <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eclipse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eclipse</strong></a> and a lunar <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eclipse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eclipse</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">日历列出了日食和月食的日期。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Astronomers used the calendar to plan observations of both a solar and a lunar <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eclipse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eclipse</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">天文学家使用日历安排日食和月食的观测。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A lunar <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eclipse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eclipse</strong></a> differs from a solar <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eclipse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eclipse</strong></a>, though both can be predicted and marked on a calendar.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">月食与日食不同，但两者都能预测并标在日历上。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">observe an eclipse</span> · 观测日月食</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=orbit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">orbit</a></span> · 轨道</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alignment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">alignment</a></span> · 排列成线</li></ul>
