<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-202">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-202" class="vocabulary-word-title">dusk<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-202" aria-label="定位 dusk">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-202" data-mark-label="dusk" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dusk：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dusk：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dusk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dusk" title="在有道查询 dusk">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-202</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 黄昏，傍晚</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We left at sunrise and returned after sunset, when <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dusk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dusk</strong></a> softened the hills.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们日出时出发，日落后返回，此时暮色柔化了群山。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Bird counts began at sunrise and resumed around sunset before <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dusk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dusk</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">鸟类计数从日出开始，并在日落前后的黄昏来临前恢复。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The camera recorded sunrise, sunset and the changing colours of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dusk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dusk</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">相机记录了日出、日落及黄昏变换的色彩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">at dusk</span> · 黄昏时</p>

</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-203">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-203" class="vocabulary-word-title">heaven<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-203" aria-label="定位 heaven">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-203" data-mark-label="heaven" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="heaven：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="heaven：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heaven&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 heaven" title="在有道查询 heaven">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-203</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 天堂；极乐(之地)；[the ~s] 天空</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">With warm sunshine and clear water, the island felt like paradise and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heaven&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>heaven</strong></a> on earth.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">阳光温暖、海水清澈，这座岛宛如乐园和人间天堂。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For tired hikers, sunshine over the valley made the campsite seem like paradise, almost <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heaven&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>heaven</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">对疲惫的徒步者而言，山谷上方的阳光使营地宛如乐园，近似天堂。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Tourism brochures promise sunshine and paradise, but preserving this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heaven&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>heaven</strong></a> on earth requires careful management.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旅游宣传册承诺阳光与乐园，但保护这处人间天堂需要精心管理。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=idyllic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">idyllic</a></span> · 田园诗般的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=serenity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">serenity</a></span> · 宁静</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

</details>

### 词群 01.18｜水与空气怎样运动 {#group-01-18}

**串联方式：** 从蒸发、循环和降水回到露水，再观察滴流、涌流、吹拂、漂浮等运动；drown 接到涉水风险。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-208">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-208" class="vocabulary-word-title">vapour<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-208" aria-label="定位 vapour">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-208" data-mark-label="vapour" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="vapour：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="vapour：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vapour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 vapour" title="在有道查询 vapour">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-208</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 蒸气；水汽</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Water can evaporate into <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vapour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vapour</strong></a>, circulate through the atmosphere and precipitate as rain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">水可以蒸发为水汽，在大气中循环，并以雨的形式降落。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Heat makes water evaporate; the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vapour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vapour</strong></a> can circulate before cooling and forming droplets that precipitate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">热使水蒸发，水汽循环后冷却，形成降落的水滴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the model, water will evaporate, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vapour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vapour</strong></a> will circulate, and condensed droplets will precipitate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模型中，水会蒸发，水汽会循环，凝结的水滴会降落。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">water vapour</span> · 水蒸气</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=condensation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">condensation</a></span> · 凝结</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=water%20cycle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">water cycle</a></span> · 水循环</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Heat makes water <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evaporate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>evaporate</strong></a>; the vapour can circulate before cooling and forming droplets that precipitate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">热使水蒸发，水汽循环后冷却，形成降落的水滴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the model, water will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evaporate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>evaporate</strong></a>, vapour will circulate, and condensed droplets will precipitate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模型中，水会蒸发，水汽会循环，凝结的水滴会降落。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">evaporate quickly</span> · 迅速蒸发</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Heat makes water evaporate; the vapour can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=circulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>circulate</strong></a> before cooling and forming droplets that precipitate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">热使水蒸发，水汽循环后冷却，形成降落的水滴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the model, water will evaporate, vapour will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=circulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>circulate</strong></a>, and condensed droplets will precipitate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模型中，水会蒸发，水汽会循环，凝结的水滴会降落。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Heat makes water evaporate; the vapour can circulate before cooling and forming droplets that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=precipitate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>precipitate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">热使水蒸发，水汽循环后冷却，形成降落的水滴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the model, water will evaporate, vapour will circulate, and condensed droplets will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=precipitate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>precipitate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模型中，水会蒸发，水汽会循环，凝结的水滴会降落。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-216">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-216" class="vocabulary-word-title">dew<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-216" aria-label="定位 dew">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-216" data-mark-label="dew" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dew：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dew：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dew" title="在有道查询 dew">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-216</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 露水</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Dew</strong></a> began to drip as rain started to pour, forcing crews to drain a pool where small animals could drown.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雨开始倾泻时露水滴落，迫使工作人员排空可能使小动物溺水的水池。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dew</strong></a> drip from leaves, then saw rain pour into a blocked drain where insects might drown.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看着露水从叶片滴落，随后看雨水涌入堵塞排水口，昆虫可能在那里淹死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After collecting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dew</strong></a>, let excess water drip away and pour the rest into a safe drain so trapped insects do not drown.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收集露水后，让多余水滴走，将剩余水倒入安全排水口，避免被困昆虫淹死。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">morning dew</span> · 晨露</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=runoff&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">runoff</a></span> · 径流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drowning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">drowning</a></span> · 溺水</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-217">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-217" class="vocabulary-word-title">pour<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-217" aria-label="定位 pour">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-217" data-mark-label="pour" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pour：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pour：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pour" title="在有道查询 pour">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-217</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 倾泻；倒；(雨)倾盆而下</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Dew began to drip as rain started to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pour</strong></a>, forcing crews to drain a pool where small animals could drown.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雨开始倾泻时露水滴落，迫使工作人员排空可能使小动物溺水的水池。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched dew drip from leaves, then saw rain <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pour</strong></a> into a blocked drain where insects might drown.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看着露水从叶片滴落，随后看雨水涌入堵塞排水口，昆虫可能在那里淹死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After collecting dew, let excess water drip away and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pour</strong></a> the rest into a safe drain so trapped insects do not drown.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收集露水后，让多余水滴走，将剩余水倒入安全排水口，避免被困昆虫淹死。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pour water</span> · 倒水</p>

</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-219">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-219" class="vocabulary-word-title">drip<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-219" aria-label="定位 drip">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-219" data-mark-label="drip" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="drip：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="drip：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 drip" title="在有道查询 drip">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-219</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 滴出(液体)；滴下</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Dew began to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drip</strong></a> as rain started to pour, forcing crews to drain a pool where small animals could drown.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雨开始倾泻时露水滴落，迫使工作人员排空可能使小动物溺水的水池。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched dew <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drip</strong></a> from leaves, then saw rain pour into a blocked drain where insects might drown.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看着露水从叶片滴落，随后看雨水涌入堵塞排水口，昆虫可能在那里淹死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After collecting dew, let excess water <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drip</strong></a> away and pour the rest into a safe drain so trapped insects do not drown.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收集露水后，让多余水滴走，将剩余水倒入安全排水口，避免被困昆虫淹死。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a slow drip</span> · 缓慢滴水</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=runoff&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">runoff</a></span> · 径流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drowning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">drowning</a></span> · 溺水</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-220">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-220" class="vocabulary-word-title">drown<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-220" aria-label="定位 drown">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-220" data-mark-label="drown" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="drown：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="drown：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drown&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 drown" title="在有道查询 drown">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-220</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(使)淹死；浸泡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Dew began to drip as rain started to pour, forcing crews to drain a pool where small animals could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drown&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drown</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雨开始倾泻时露水滴落，迫使工作人员排空可能使小动物溺水的水池。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched dew drip from leaves, then saw rain pour into a blocked drain where insects might <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drown&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drown</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看着露水从叶片滴落，随后看雨水涌入堵塞排水口，昆虫可能在那里淹死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After collecting dew, let excess water drip away and pour the rest into a safe drain so trapped insects do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drown&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drown</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收集露水后，让多余水滴走，将剩余水倒入安全排水口，避免被困昆虫淹死。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">drown in water</span> · 溺水</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=runoff&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">runoff</a></span> · 径流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drowning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">drowning</a></span> · 溺水</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-221">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-221" class="vocabulary-word-title">blow<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-221" aria-label="定位 blow">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-221" data-mark-label="blow" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="blow：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="blow：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 blow" title="在有道查询 blow">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-221</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 吹；(风)吹；吹动；n. 用力的一击；打击，挫折</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A puff of wind began to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blow</strong></a> across the spring as water continued to gush from the rock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一阵风开始吹过泉眼，水继续从岩石中涌出。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We heard the wind <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blow</strong></a>, saw steam puff from a vent and watched hot water gush nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们听风吹，看蒸汽从通气口喷出，热水在附近涌流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blow</strong></a> into the tube with a sudden puff, water will gush from the other end.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若你朝管中猛吹一口气，水就会从另一端喷出。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">blow strongly</span> · 猛烈吹动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=airflow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">airflow</a></span> · 气流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">surge</a></span> · 涌动</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-222">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-222" class="vocabulary-word-title">puff<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-222" aria-label="定位 puff">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-222" data-mark-label="puff" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="puff：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="puff：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=puff&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 puff" title="在有道查询 puff">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-222</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 喷出；喘息；n.(吹出的)一股，一缕</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=puff&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>puff</strong></a> of wind began to blow across the spring as water continued to gush from the rock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一阵风开始吹过泉眼，水继续从岩石中涌出。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We heard the wind blow, saw steam <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=puff&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>puff</strong></a> from a vent and watched hot water gush nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们听风吹，看蒸汽从通气口喷出，热水在附近涌流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you blow into the tube with a sudden <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=puff&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>puff</strong></a>, water will gush from the other end.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若你朝管中猛吹一口气，水就会从另一端喷出。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a puff of wind</span> · 一阵风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=airflow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">airflow</a></span> · 气流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">surge</a></span> · 涌动</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-223">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-223" class="vocabulary-word-title">gush<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-223" aria-label="定位 gush">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-223" data-mark-label="gush" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="gush：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="gush：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gush&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 gush" title="在有道查询 gush">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-223</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.涌出；n. 涌出</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A puff of wind began to blow across the spring as water continued to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gush&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gush</strong></a> from the rock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一阵风开始吹过泉眼，水继续从岩石中涌出。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We heard the wind blow, saw steam puff from a vent and watched hot water <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gush&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gush</strong></a> nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们听风吹，看蒸汽从通气口喷出，热水在附近涌流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you blow into the tube with a sudden puff, water will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gush&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gush</strong></a> from the other end.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若你朝管中猛吹一口气，水就会从另一端喷出。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">gush from a pipe</span> · 从管中喷涌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=airflow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">airflow</a></span> · 气流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">surge</a></span> · 涌动</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-227">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-227" class="vocabulary-word-title">emerge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-227" aria-label="定位 emerge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-227" data-mark-label="emerge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="emerge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="emerge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emerge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 emerge" title="在有道查询 emerge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-227</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 浮现，露出；摆脱出来；暴露</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A flash illuminated the lake as we watched a log <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emerge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>emerge</strong></a> and float downstream.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一道闪光照亮湖面，我们看着原木浮出水面并顺流漂去。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fish <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emerge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>emerge</strong></a> beneath plants that float on the pond, then disappear in a flash.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">鱼从漂浮在池塘上的植物下出现，转瞬消失。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After a flash of lightning, shapes began to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emerge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>emerge</strong></a> from the mist and float across the dark water.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">闪电过后，一些轮廓开始从薄雾中显现，漂过黑暗水面。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">emerge from water</span> · 从水中出现</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=buoyancy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">buoyancy</a></span> · 浮力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反射</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-228">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-228" class="vocabulary-word-title">flash<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-228" aria-label="定位 flash">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-228" data-mark-label="flash" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="flash：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="flash：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 flash" title="在有道查询 flash">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-228</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(使)闪光；(使)闪现；(通过计算机、卫星等)快速发送；n. 闪光(灯)；闪现</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flash</strong></a> illuminated the lake as we watched a log emerge and float downstream.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一道闪光照亮湖面，我们看着原木浮出水面并顺流漂去。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fish emerge beneath plants that float on the pond, then disappear in a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flash</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">鱼从漂浮在池塘上的植物下出现，转瞬消失。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flash</strong></a> of lightning, shapes began to emerge from the mist and float across the dark water.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">闪电过后，一些轮廓开始从薄雾中显现，漂过黑暗水面。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a flash of light</span> · 一道闪光</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=buoyancy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">buoyancy</a></span> · 浮力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反射</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-229">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-229" class="vocabulary-word-title">float<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-229" aria-label="定位 float">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-229" data-mark-label="float" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="float：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="float：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=float&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 float" title="在有道查询 float">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-229</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 漂浮；飘浮</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A flash illuminated the lake as we watched a log emerge and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=float&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>float</strong></a> downstream.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一道闪光照亮湖面，我们看着原木浮出水面并顺流漂去。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fish emerge beneath plants that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=float&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>float</strong></a> on the pond, then disappear in a flash.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">鱼从漂浮在池塘上的植物下出现，转瞬消失。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After a flash of lightning, shapes began to emerge from the mist and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=float&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>float</strong></a> across the dark water.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">闪电过后，一些轮廓开始从薄雾中显现，漂过黑暗水面。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">float on water</span> · 漂浮在水上</p>

</div>
</details>

### 词群 01.19｜概括环境与强度 {#group-01-19}

**串联方式：** 描述周围环境和所处条件，再区分天然、人造、合成，以及密度和强度。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-224">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-224" class="vocabulary-word-title">dense<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-224" aria-label="定位 dense">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-224" data-mark-label="dense" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dense：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dense：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dense&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dense" title="在有道查询 dense">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-224</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rainfall intensity affected <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dense&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dense</strong></a> vegetation differently from nearby land under intensive farming.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">降雨强度对茂密植被的影响不同于附近的集约农业用地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Intensive fieldwork measured light intensity beneath a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dense&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dense</strong></a> forest canopy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">密集的野外工作测量了茂密林冠下的光照强度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dense&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Dense</strong></a> planting under intensive cultivation can reduce the intensity of light reaching the soil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">集约栽培中的密植可能降低到达土壤的光照强度。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">dense vegetation</span> · 茂密植被</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=density&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">density</a></span> · 密度</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intensify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intensify</a></span> · 增强</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-230">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report describes the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=environment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>environment</strong></a>, the condition of surrounding land and the current water situation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告描述了环境、周边土地状况及当前水资源局面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A change in the surrounding <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=environment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>environment</strong></a> worsened the river's condition and created an urgent situation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">周围环境的变化使河流状况恶化，造成紧急局面。</p></li>

</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report describes the environment, the condition of surrounding land and the current water <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=situation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>situation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告描述了环境、周边土地状况及当前水资源局面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A change in the surrounding environment worsened the river's condition and created an urgent <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=situation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>situation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">周围环境的变化使河流状况恶化，造成紧急局面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To understand this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=situation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>situation</strong></a>, assess the river's condition and the surrounding environment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要理解这一局面，需评估河流状况及周围环境。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a difficult situation</span> · 困难局面</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surroundings&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">surroundings</a></span> · 周围环境</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=context&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">context</a></span> · 背景</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-234">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-234" class="vocabulary-word-title">nature<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-234" aria-label="定位 nature">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-234" data-mark-label="nature" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="nature：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="nature：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 nature" title="在有道查询 nature">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-234</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 大自然；本性；性质</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit contrasts natural materials found in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nature</strong></a> with artificial products made from synthetic fibres.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将自然界中的天然材料与合成纤维制成的人造产品比较。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Synthetic fibres and artificial dyes can affect <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nature</strong></a> when they replace biodegradable natural materials.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合成纤维和人工染料替代可降解天然材料时可能影响自然环境。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A natural appearance does not prove a product comes from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nature</strong></a> rather than artificial or synthetic processes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">外观天然并不能证明产品来自自然，而非人工或合成工艺。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">protect nature</span> · 保护自然</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=man-made&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">man-made</a></span> · 人造的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=organic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">organic</a></span> · 有机的</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-235">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-235" class="vocabulary-word-title">natural<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-235" aria-label="定位 natural">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-235" data-mark-label="natural" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="natural：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="natural：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=natural&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 natural" title="在有道查询 natural">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-235</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 自然的；天然的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit contrasts <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=natural&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>natural</strong></a> materials found in nature with artificial products made from synthetic fibres.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将自然界中的天然材料与合成纤维制成的人造产品比较。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Synthetic fibres and artificial dyes can affect nature when they replace biodegradable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=natural&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>natural</strong></a> materials.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合成纤维和人工染料替代可降解天然材料时可能影响自然环境。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=natural&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>natural</strong></a> appearance does not prove a product comes from nature rather than artificial or synthetic processes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">外观天然并不能证明产品来自自然，而非人工或合成工艺。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">natural materials</span> · 天然材料</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=man-made&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">man-made</a></span> · 人造的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=organic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">organic</a></span> · 有机的</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Synthetic fibres and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=artificial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>artificial</strong></a> dyes can affect nature when they replace biodegradable natural materials.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合成纤维和人工染料替代可降解天然材料时可能影响自然环境。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A natural appearance does not prove a product comes from nature rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=artificial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>artificial</strong></a> or synthetic processes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">外观天然并不能证明产品来自自然，而非人工或合成工艺。</p></li>

### 词群 01.20｜从能源连接环境议题 {#group-01-20}

**串联方式：** 辨认汽油、气体与石油的词形，把燃料使用接到排放和污染讨论。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-238">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-238" class="vocabulary-word-title">petrol<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-238" aria-label="定位 petrol">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-238" data-mark-label="petrol" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="petrol：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="petrol：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=petrol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 petrol" title="在有道查询 petrol">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-238</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [ 英 ] 汽油</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=petrol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Petrol</strong></a>, also called gasoline, is refined from petroleum, while natural gas is a different fuel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">汽油也称gasoline，由石油炼制而成，天然气则是另一种燃料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rising petroleum prices pushed up <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=petrol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>petrol</strong></a> and gasoline costs, while natural gas followed a separate market.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">石油涨价推高了汽油成本，而天然气遵循另一个市场。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The refinery processes petroleum into <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=petrol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>petrol</strong></a> or gasoline and also handles gas produced during refining.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">炼油厂将石油加工为汽油，并处理炼制过程中产生的气体。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">unleaded petrol</span> · 无铅汽油</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refinery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">refinery</a></span> · 炼油厂</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fossil%20fuel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fossil fuel</a></span> · 化石燃料</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-239">
<summary><h4 id="word-01-239" class="vocabulary-word-title">gas<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-01-239" aria-label="定位 gas">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="01-239" data-mark-label="gas" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="gas：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="gas：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gas&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 gas" title="在有道查询 gas">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">01-239</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 气体；[ 美 ] 汽油</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Petrol, also called gasoline, is refined from petroleum, while natural <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gas&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gas</strong></a> is a different fuel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">汽油也称gasoline，由石油炼制而成，天然气则是另一种燃料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rising petroleum prices pushed up petrol and gasoline costs, while natural <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gas&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gas</strong></a> followed a separate market.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">石油涨价推高了汽油成本，而天然气遵循另一个市场。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The refinery processes petroleum into petrol or gasoline and also handles <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gas&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gas</strong></a> produced during refining.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">炼油厂将石油加工为汽油，并处理炼制过程中产生的气体。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">natural gas</span> · 天然气</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refinery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">refinery</a></span> · 炼油厂</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fossil%20fuel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fossil fuel</a></span> · 化石燃料</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="01-240">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [ 美 ] 汽油</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Petrol, also called <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gasoline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gasoline</strong></a>, is refined from petroleum, while natural gas is a different fuel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">汽油也称gasoline，由石油炼制而成，天然气则是另一种燃料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rising petroleum prices pushed up petrol and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gasoline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gasoline</strong></a> costs, while natural gas followed a separate market.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">石油涨价推高了汽油成本，而天然气遵循另一个市场。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The refinery processes petroleum into petrol or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gasoline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gasoline</strong></a> and also handles gas produced during refining.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">炼油厂将石油加工为汽油，并处理炼制过程中产生的气体。</p></li>
</ol>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **carbon dioxide emissions**：二氧化碳排放。
- **soil erosion**：土壤侵蚀；erosion 是 erode 的相关名词。
- **protect the natural environment**：保护自然环境。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

The climate affects the natural environment in many ways. Drought can reduce the amount of water in a reservoir, while heavy rainfall can cause flooding. When people deforest a hillside, the soil may erode more easily. Understanding these connections helps us describe environmental problems clearly.

**中文理解：** 气候以多种方式影响自然环境。干旱会减少水库中的水量，强降雨则可能造成洪水。人们砍伐山坡上的树木后，土壤可能更容易受到侵蚀。理解这些联系，可以帮助我们清楚描述环境问题。
